# Samantapāsādikā Vol. 1

> **Category:** Aṭṭhakathā
> **Text:** Samantapāsādikā Vol. 1
> **Edition:** Pali Text Society (PTS)

---


<!-- PTS Page 001 -->
<a id="page-001"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 001]*

<p align="center">SAMANTAPĀSĀDIKĀ NĀMA
                              VINAYAṬṬHAKATHĀ
               Namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa.
                    Yo kappakoṭīhi pi appameyyaṃ
                    kālaṃ karonto atidukkarāṇi[^001-1]
                    khedaṃ gato lokahitāya nātho,
                    namo mahākāruṇikassa tassa.  // Sp_0.1 //
                    asambuddhaṃ buddhanisevitaṃ yaṃ
                    bhavābhavaṃ gacchati jīvaloko,
                    namo avijjādikilesajāla-
                    vidhaṃsino dhammavarassa tassa.  // Sp_0.2 //
                    guṇehi yo sīlasamādhipaññā-
                    vimuttiñāṇappabhutīhi yutto,
                    khettaṃ janānaṃ kusalatthikānaṃ
                    tam ariyasaṃghaṃ sirasā namāmi.  // Sp_0.3 //
                    icc evam accantanamassaneyyaṃ[^001-2]
                    namassamāno ratanattayaṃ yaṃ,
                    puññābhisandaṃ vipulaṃ alatthaṃ
                    tassānubhāvena hatantarāyo.  // Sp_0.4 //
                    yasmiṃ ṭhite sāsanam aṭṭhitassa
                    patiṭṭhitaṃ hoti susaṇṭhitassa,
                    taṃ vaṇṇayissaṃ vinayaṃ amissaṃ
                    nissāya pubbācariyānubhāvaṃ.  // Sp_0.5 //
                    kāmañ ca pubbācariyāsabhehi
                    ñāṇambuniddhotamalāsavehi
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
1 Sp.B.B1.B2.Bp. -karāni. 
2 Sp.-namassanīyaṃ.</p>



<!-- PTS Page 002 -->
<a id="page-002"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 002]*

visuddhavijjāpaṭisambhidehi
                    saddhammasaṃvaṇṇanakovidehi,  // Sp_0.6 //
                    sallekhiye no sulabhūpamehi
                    Mahāvihārassa dhajūpamehi,
                    saṃvaṇṇito 'yaṃ vinayo nayehi
                    cittehi sambuddhavaranvayehi.  // Sp_0.7 //
                    saṃvaṇṇanā Sīharadīpakena[^002-1]
                    vākyena esā pana saṅkhaṭattā,[^002-2]
                    na kiñci atthaṃ abhisambhuṇāti[^002-3]
                    dīpantare bhikkhujanassa yasmā,  // Sp_0.8 //
                    tasmā imaṃ pālinayānurūpaṃ[^002-4]
                    saṃvaṇṇanaṃ dāni samārabhissaṃ
                    ajjhesanaṃ Buddhasirivhayassa
                    therassa sammā samanussaranto.  // Sp_0.9 //
                    saṃvaṇṇanaṃ tañ ca samārabhanto[^002-5]
                    tasmā[^002-6] Mahāaṭṭhakathaṃ sarīraṃ
                    katvā Mahāpaccariyaṃ tatheva
                    Kurundināmādisu[^002-7] vissutāsu  // Sp_0.10 //
                    vinicchayo aṭṭhakathāsu vutto
                    yo yuttam atthaṃ apariccajanto,
                    tato pi antogadhatheravādaṃ
                    saṃvaṇṇanaṃ sammasamārabhissaṃ.  // Sp_0.11 //
                    taṃ me nisāmentu pasannacittā
                    therā ca bhikkhū navamajjhimā ca
                    dhammappadīpassa tathāgatassa
                    sakkacca[^002-8] dhammaṃ paṭimānayantā[^002-9]:  // Sp_0.12 //
                    buddhena dhammo vinayo ca vutto
                    yo tassa puttehi tatheva ñāto,
                    so yehi tesaṃ matim accajantā
                    yasmā pure aṭṭhakathā akaṃsu,  // Sp_0.13 //
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
1 S.B.B1.B2.Bp. Sīhaḷa-.
2 B.B1.B2.Bp. saṅkhata-.
3 Sp.B.B1.-bhunāti.
4 S.B.B1.B2.Bp. pāḷi-.
5 B.sammāra-. 
6 S.B.B1.B2.Bp. tassā.
7 B.B1.Bp. -ādīsu.
8 B. sakkaccaṃ.
9 S.Sp. pati-.

##### *[PTS Page 003]*

tasmā hi yaṃ aṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ
                    taṃ vajjayitvāna pamādalekhaṃ,
                    sabbam pi sikkhāsu sagāravānaṃ
                    yasmā pamāṇaṃ idha paṇḍitāna  // Sp_0.14 //
                    tato ca bhāsantaram eva hitvā
                    vitthāramaggañ ca samāsayitvā,
                    vinicchayaṃ sabbam asesayitvā
                    tantikkamaṃ kañci[^003-1] avokkamitvā,  // Sp_0.15 //
                    suttantikānaṃ vacanānam atthaṃ
                    suttānurūpaṃ paridīpayantī,[^003-2]
                    yasmā ayaṃ hessati vaṇṇanā pi
                    sakkacca tasmā anusikkhitabbā ti.[^003-3]  // Sp_0.16 //
     Tattha taṃ vaṇṇayissaṃ vinayan ti vuttattā vinayo
tāva vavatthapetabbo, ten' etaṃ vuccati: vinayo nāma idha
sakalaṃ Vinayapiṭakaṃ adhippetaṃ. saṃvaṇṇanatthaṃ pan'
assa ayaṃ mātikā:
          vuttaṃ yena yadā yasmā dhāritaṃ yena cābhataṃ,
          yattha-ppatiṭṭhitaṃ c'etametaṃ vatvā vidhiṃ tato,
          tenā 'ti ādipāṭhassa atthaṃ nānappakārato
          dassayanto karissāmi vinayass' atthavaṇṇanan 'ti.
tattha vuttaṃ yena yadā yasmā cā 'ti[^003-4] idam tāva vacanaṃ
tena samayena buddho bhagavā Verañjāyaṃ viharatīti evam-
ādivacanaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ. idaṃ hi buddhassa bha-
gavato attapaccakkhavacanaṃ[^003-5] na hoti, tasmā vattabbam
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
1 B.B1.B2.Bp. kiñci.
2 S.Sp.B.B1.B2. -yanti.
3 The Chinese translation AḌ. 489 omits the verses 6-16 chiefly
     referring to the sīhala atthakathā, while it adds 6 other verses which
     are not found in Pāli.  The general purport is something like this: (I
     expound the meaning of Vinaya) ‘so that the good law may long
     remain in the world and all beings fully enjoy its benefit. By the merit
     of this I desire to dispel all evils (hatantarāyo).  If  one wishes to 
     keep the precept (sīla) one should keep it (until one is) freed from all
     sufferings.' As to vv.4 and 5, only the first half of 4 and the second
     half of 5 are reproduced in Chinese, though not precisely.
4 Sp.B.B1.B2.Bp. yasmā ti.
5 S. paccakka-.

##### *[PTS Page 004]*

ev' etaṃ[^004-1] idaṃ vacanaṃ kena[^004-2] vuttam, kadā kasmā ca
vuttan ti.[^004-3] āyasmatā Upālittherena vuttaṃ taṃ ca pana
paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikāle. paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti[^004-4] nāma c'
esā kiñcāpi pañcasatikasaṅgītikkhandhake suvuttā,[^004-5] nidāna-
kosallatthaṃ pana idhāpi iminā nayena veditabbā.
     dhammacakkappavattanaṃ[^004-6] hi ādiṃ katvā yāva Su-
bhaddaparibbājakavinayanā katabuddhakicce Kusinārāyaṃ
Upavattane[^004-7] Mallānaṃ sālavane yamakasālānam antare
Visākhapuṇṇamadivase[^004-8] paccūsasamaye[^004-9] anupādisesāya nib-
bānadhātuyā[^004-10] parinibbute bhagavati lokanāthe bhagavato
parinibbāne[^004-11] sannipatitānaṃ sattannaṃ bhikkhusatasahas-
sānaṃ saṃghatthero āyasmā Mahākassapo sattāhaparinib-
bute bhagavati, Subhaddena buḍḍhapabbajitena[^004-12]: alaṃ
āvuso mā socittha mā paridevittha, sumuttā mayaṃ tena
mahāsamaṇena, upaddutā ca homa idaṃ vo kappati idam
vo[^004-13] na kappatīti, idāni pana mayaṃ yaṃ icchissāma taṃ
karissāma, yaṃ na icchissāma taṃ na[^004-14] karissāmā'ti vutta-
vacanam anussaranto, ṭhānaṃ[^004-15] kho pan' etaṃ vijjati yaṃ
pāpabhikkhū[^004-16] atītasatthukaṃ pāvacanan ti maññamānā
pakkhaṃ labhitvā na cirass' eva[^004-17] saddhammaṃ antara-
dhāpeyyuṃ. yāva ca dhammavinayo tiṭṭhati tāva anatīta-
satthukam eva pāvacanaṃ hoti.[^004-18] vuttaṃ h' etaṃ bhaga-
vatā: yo vo mayā Ānanda[^004-19] dhammo ca vinayo ca desito
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
1 S.eva taṃ; B.B1.B2.Bp. omit ev'.
2 S.tena.
3 B.B1. kadā vuttaṃ, kasmā ca vuttan ti; B2. kadā ca vuttaṃ,
     kasmā ca vuttan ti; Bp. kadā vuttaṃ, kasmā vuttan ti.
4 B2. saṃgiti, sic passim. 
5 B.B1.B2.Bp. vuttā.
6 From here to p. 104 ābhatan ti has been edited by H. Oldenberg
     in his Vinaya, iii, 283 f., London,1881.
7 Sum. Upavattaṃ. 
8 B.B1. Visākhā-.
9 B2. paccusa-.
10 Sp. nibbāṇa-; B.parinibbāna-, sic passim.
11 Sum. dhātubhājanadivase  for parinibbāne.
12 Ch. puts mahalla for buḍḍha-.
13 Sp. omits vo.
14 S.Sp.B.B1.B2.Bp. na taṃ.
15 Sum. inserts before ṭhānaṃ, ‘edisassa ca saṃghasannipātassa puna
     dullabhabhāvaṃ maññamāno.'
16 S.B.B1.-bhikkhu atīta-.
17 B2. cīrass' eva.
18 S1.(D).Sum. pāvacanan ti.
19 S.Sp.B2.Sum. yo vo Ānanda mayā; Bp. yo kho Ānanda mayā.

##### *[PTS Page 005]*

paññatto so vo[^005-1] mam' accayena satthā ti. yan nūnāhaṃ
dhammañ ca vinayañ ca saṃgāyeyyaṃ yathāyidaṃ[^005-2] sāsa-
naṃ addhaniyaṃ assa ciraṭṭhitikaṃ.[^005-3] yaṃ cāhaṃ bhaga-
vatā: dhāressasi pana tvaṃ me[^005-4] Kassapa sānāni[^005-5] paṃsukū-
lāni[^005-6] nibbasanānīti vatvā civare sādhāraṇaparibhogena
anuggahito[^005-7]: ahaṃ bhikkhave yāvad eva ākaṅkhāmi vivicc'
eva kāmehi --pe-- paṭhamajjhānaṃ[^005-8] upasampajja viharāmi,
Kassapo pi bhikkhave yāvad eva ākaṅkhati vivicc' eva kā-
mehi --pe-- paṭhamajjhānaṃ[^005-8] upasampajja viharatīti evam-
ādinā nayena navānupubbavihārachaḷabhiññādibhede[^005-9] uttari-
manussadhamme attanā samasamaṭṭhapanena[^005-10] ca anug-
gahito,[^005-11] tassa[^005-12] me[^005-13] kiṃ aññaṃ ānaṇyaṃ[^005-14] bhavissati. nanu
maṃ bhagavā rājā viya sakakavacaissariyānuppadānena[^005-15]
attano kulavaṃsapatiṭṭhāpakaṃ[^005-16] puttaṃ saddhammavaṃ-
sapatiṭṭhāpako[^005-16] me ayaṃ bhavissatīti mantvā iminā asā-
dhāraṇena anuggahena anuggahesīti[^005-17] cintayanto, dhamma-
vinayasaṃgāyanatthaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ussāhaṃ janesi.[^005-18]
     yathāha: atha kho āyasmā Mahākassapo bhikkhū āma-
ntesi[^005-19]: ekam idāhaṃ āvuso samayaṃ Pāvāya Kusināraṃ[^005-20]
addhānamaggapaṭipanno[^005-21] mahatā bhikkhusaṃghena sad-
dhiṃ pañcamattehi bhikkhusatehīti sabbaṃ Subhaddakaṇḍaṃ
vitthārato veditabbaṃ.[^005-22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
1 S. yo vo. 
2  S.Sp.B1.B2.Sum. yathayidaṃ.
3 B.B1. cīraṭhītikaṃ; B2. cīraṭṭhitikaṃ.
4 S.Sp.B2.Bp.Sum. pana me tvaṃ. 
5 B.B1.B2.Bp.sāṇāni.
6 B2. -kulāni.
7 S.Sp.B2.Bp. omit anuggahito; Sp.Bp. however put c'eva instead.
8 S.Sp.B.B1.B2.Bp. paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ.
9 B.B1.-chaḷābhi- ; B2.-chaḷabhiññāpabhede; Bp.-chaḷabhiññāppa-
     bhede; Sum. -vihārasamāpattichaḷabhiññāppabhede.
10 Sum. -ṭṭhānena. 
11 S.Sp. -ggahīto.
12 Sum.adds before tassa ‘yathā ākāse pāniṃ cāletvā alaggacittatāya
     c' eva candopamapaṭipadāya ca pasaṃsito.'
13 B.B1.B2.Bp. omit me.
14 S. ānanyaṃ; B2.Bp.āṇaṇyaṃ.
15 Sp. sakkacca issari-. 
16 Bp.-ppaṭiṭṭh-.
17 Sum. omits iti and adds ‘imāya ca uḷārāya pasaṃsāya pasaṃsīti.'
18 B.Bp. janeti. 
19 S. āmantehi.
20 S1.(D).Sum.Kusinārāyaṃ. 
21 Bp.-ppaṭipanno.
22 Sum. adds‘atthaṃ pan'assa mahāparinibbānāvasāne āgataṭṭhāne
     eva kathayissāma.'

tato paraṃ āha: handa mayaṃ āvuso dhammañ ca
vinayañ ca saṃgāyāma[^006-1] pure adhammo dippati[^006-2] dhammo
paṭibāhīyati,[^006-3] avinayo dippati[^006-2] vinayo paṭibāhīyati,[^006-3] pure
adhammavādino balavanto honti dhammavādino dubbalā
honti, avinayavādino balavanto honti vinayavādino dubbalā
hontīti. bhikkhū āhaṃsu[^006-4]: tena hi bhante thero bhikkhū
uccinatū 'ti.
     thero sakalanavaṅgasatthusāsanapariyattidhare puthujja-
nasotāpannasakadāgāmianāgāmisukkhavipassakakhīṇāsava-
bhikkhū anekasate anekasahasse ca[^006-5] vajjetvā[^006-6] tipiṭakasab-
bapariyattippabhedadhare[^006-7] paṭisambhidāppatte mahānu-
bhāve yebhuyyena bhagavatā etadaggaṃ āropite tevijjādi-
bhede khīṇāsavabhikkhū yeva ekūnapañcasate pariggahesi.
ye sandhāya idaṃ vuttaṃ: atha kho āyasmā Mahākassapo
eken' ūnapañcārahantasatāni uccinīti.[^006-8]
     kissa pana thero eken' ūnam akāsīti. āyasmato Ānan-
dattherassa okāsakaraṇatthaṃ. tena h'[^006-9] āyasmatā sahāpi
vināpi na sakkā[^006-10] dhammasaṃgīti[^006-11] kātuṃ. so h' āyasmā
sekho[^006-12] sakaraṇīyo, tasmā saha[^006-13] na sakkā. yasmā pan' assa
kiñci dasabaladesitaṃ[^006-14] suttaṃ geyyādikaṃ[^006-15] [^006-16]bhagavato
asammukhā patiggahiṭaṃ[^006-16] nāma n'atthi,[^006-17] tasmā vinā pi[^006-18]
na sakkā. yadi evaṃ sekho[^006-12] pi samāno dhammasaṃgītiyā
bahūpakārattā[^006-19] therena[^006-20] uccinitabbo assa. atha kasmā[^006-21] na

##### *[PTS Page 007]*

uccinito ti[^007-1] parūpavādavivajjanato.[^007-2] thero hi āyasmante
Ānande ativiya vissattho ahosi tathā hi naṃ sirasmiṃ pa-
litesu[^007-3] jātesu pi: na v' āyaṃ kumārako mattam aññāsīti,
kumārakavādena ovadati.[^007-4] Sakyakulappasūto[^007-5] cāyaṃ āyas-
mā[^007-6] tathāgatassa bhātā cūlapituputto,[^007-7] tatra hi[^007-8] bhikkhū
chandāgamanaṃ viya maññamānā bahū[^007-9] asekha-[^007-10] paṭisam-
bhidappatte[^007-11] bhikkhū ṭhapetvā Ānandaṃ sekha-[^007-12] paṭisam-
bhidappattaṃ[^007-13] thero uccinīti upavadeyyum. taṃ parūpa-
vādaṃ parivajjento[^007-14]:Ānandaṃ vinā saṃgīti[^007-15] na sakkā kātum,
bhikkhūnaṃ yevānumatiyā[^007-16] gahessāmīti taṃ[^007-17] na uccini.[^007-18]
     atha sayaṃ eva bhikkhū Ānandass' atthāya[^007-19] theraṃ yāci-
ṃsu. yathāha: bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ Mahākassapaṃ etad
avocuṃ: ayaṃ bhante āyasmā Ānando kiñcāpi sekho[^007-20] abhab-
bo[^007-21] chandā dosā mohā bhayā agatiṃ gantuṃ,[^007-22] bahu vata
tena[^007-23] bhagavato santike dhammo ca vinayo ca pariyatto.
tena hi bhante thero āyasmantaṃ pi Ānandaṃ uccinatū 'ti.
atha kho āyasmā Mahākassapo āyasmantam pi Ānandaṃ
uccini. evaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anumatiyā uccinitena tenāyas-
matā saddhim pañca therasatāni ahesuṃ.
     atha kho therānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ etad ahosi: kattha nu[^007-24]
kho mayaṃ dhammañ ca vinayañ ca saṃgāyeyyāmā 'ti.
atha kho[^007-25] therānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ etad ahosi: Rājagahaṃ

<!-- PTS Page 008 -->
<a id="page-008"></a>

##### *[PTS Page 008]*

kho mahāgocaraṃ pahūtasenāsanaṃ. yan nūna mayaṃ
Rājagahe vassaṃ vasantā[^008-1] dhammañ ca vinayañ ca saṃ-
gāyeyyāma, na aññe bhikkhū Rājagahe vassaṃ upagacchey-
yun ti. kasmā pana tesaṃ[^008-2] etad ahosi. idaṃ amhākaṃ
thāvarakammaṃ[^008-3] koci visabhāgapuggalo saṃghamajjhaṃ
pavisitvā[^008-4] ukkoṭeyyā 'ti.
     athāyasmā Mahākassapo ñattidutiyakammena[^008-5] sāvesi.
taṃ Saṃgītikkhandhake vuttanayen' eva veditabbaṃ.[^008-6]
     atha tathāgatassa parinibbānato sattasu sādhukīḷanadi-
vasesu[^008-7] sattasu ca dhātupūjādivasesu vītivattesu aḍḍhamāso
atikkanto, idāni gimhānaṃ[^008-8] diyaḍḍho māso seso upakaṭṭhā
vassūpanāyikā ti mantvā Mahākassapatthero: Rājagahaṃ
āvuso gacchāmā 'ti upaḍḍhaṃ bhikkhusaṃghaṃ gahetvā[^008-9]
ekaṃ maggaṃ gato. Anuruddhatthero pi[^008-10] upaḍḍhaṃ[^008-11] ga-
hetvā ekaṃ maggaṃ gato.
     Ānandatthero pana bhagavato pattacīvaraṃ gahetvā
bhikkhusaṃghaparivuto[^008-12] Sāvatthiṃ gantvā Rājagahaṃ gan-
tukāmo yena Sāvatthi tena cārikaṃ pakkāmi.[^008-13] Ānandat-
therena gatagataṭṭhāne mahāparidevo ahosi: bhante Ānanda
kuhiṃ satthāraṃ ṭhapetvā āgato 'sīti. anupubbena pana
Sāvatthiṃ anuppatte there ca[^008-14] bhagavato parinibbānadivase

aniccatāpaṭisaṃyuttāya[^008-15] dhammiyā kathāya taṃ mahāja-

gandhakuṭiyā dvāraṃ vivaritvā mañcapīṭhaṃ[^008-17] nīharitvā
pappoṭhetvā[^008-18] gandhakuṭiṃ sammajjitvā milātamālākaca-

##### *[PTS Page 009]*

varaṃ[^009-1] chaḍḍetvā[^009-2] mañcapīṭhaṃ atiharitvā puna yathāṭṭhāne
ṭhapetvā bhagavato ṭhitakāle karaṇīyaṃ vattaṃ sabbaṃ
akāsi. atha[^009-3] thero bhagavato parinibbānato ppabhuti[^009-4]
ṭhānanisajjabahulattā[^009-5] ussannadhātukaṃ kāyaṃ samassā-
setuṃ[^009-6] dutiyadivase khīravirecanaṃ pivitvā vihāre yeva
nisīdi. yaṃ sandhāya Subhena māṇavena[^009-7] pahitaṃ māṇa-
vakaṃ etaṃ[^009-8] avoca: akālo kho māṇavaka, atthi me ajja
bhesajjamattā pītā, app eva nāma sve pi upasaṃkameyyā-
mā 'ti. dutiyadivase Cetakattherena pacchāsamaṇena gantvā
Subhena māṇavena puṭṭho Dīghanikāye Subhasuttam nāma
dasamaṃ suttam abhāsi. atha kho[^009-9] thero Jetavanavihāre
khaṇḍaphullapaṭisaṃkharaṇaṃ kārāpetvā upakaṭṭhāya[^009-10]
vassūpanāyikāya[^009-11] Rājagahaṃ gato.
     tathā Mahākassapatthero ca[^009-12] Anuruddhatthero ca sabbaṃ
bhikkhusaṃghaṃ gahetvā Rājagaham eva gato.
     tena[^009-13] kho pana samayena Rājagahe aṭṭhārasa mahāvihārā
honti. te sabbe pi chaḍḍitapatitauklāpā[^009-14] ahesuṃ. bhaga-
vato hi parinibbāne sabbe bhikkhū attano attano pattacīva-
raṃ gahetvā vihāre ca pariveṇe[^009-15] ca chaḍḍetvā[^009-16] agamaṃsu.
tattha therā bhagavato vacanapūjanatthaṃ titthiyavādapa-
rimocanatthaṃ ca paṭhamaṃ māsaṃ khaṇḍaphullapaṭisaṃ-
kharaṇaṃ[^009-17] karomā 'ti cintesuṃ.
     titthiyā hi vadeyyuṃ[^009-18]: samaṇassa Gotamassa sāvakā
satthari ṭhite yeva vihāre paṭijaggiṃsu, parinibbute chaḍ-
ḍesun[^009-16] ti. tesaṃ vādaparimocanatthañ ca cintesun ti vuttaṃ
hoti.

<i>10                            Samantapāsādikā
</i>     vuttam pi c' etaṃ[^010-1]: atha kho therānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ etad
ahosi: bhagavatā kho āvuso khaṇḍaphullapaṭisaṃkharaṇaṃ
vaṇṇitaṃ.[^010-2] handa mayaṃ āvuso paṭhamaṃ māsaṃ khaṇḍa-
phullapaṭisaṃkharaṇaṃ karoma, majjhimaṃ māsaṃ sanni-
patitvā dhammañ ca vinayañ ca saṃgāyissāmā 'ti.[^010-3]
     te dutiyadivase gantvā rājadvāre aṭṭhaṃsu. Ajātasattu
rājā āgantvā vanditvā, kiṃ bhante āgata 'tthā 'ti[^010-4] attanā
kattabbakiccaṃ[^010-5] paṭipucchi. therā aṭṭhārasamahāvihāra-
paṭisaṃkharaṇatthāya hatthakammaṃ paṭivedesuṃ. sādhu
bhante ti rājā hatthakammakārake manusse adāsi. therā
paṭhamamāsaṃ[^010-6] sabbavihāre paṭisaṃkhārāpetvā[^010-7] rañño āro-
cesuṃ: niṭṭhitaṃ mahārāja vihārapaṭisaṃkharaṇaṃ, idāni
dhammavinayasaṃgahaṃ karomā 'ti. sādhu bhante vissat-
thā[^010-8] karotha, mayhaṃ āṇācakkaṃ, tumhākaṃ dhammacak-
kaṃ hotu, āṇāpetha me[^010-9] bhante kiṃ karomīti. dhammasa-
ṃgahaṃ[^010-10] karontānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sannisajjaṭṭhānam[^010-11] ma-
hārājā 'ti. kattha karomi bhante ti. Vebhārapabbatapasse
Sattapaṇṇiguhādvāre[^010-12] kātum yuttaṃ mahārājā 'ti.
     sādhu bhante ti kho rājā Ajātasattu Vissakammunā[^010-13]
nimmitasadisaṃ suvibhattabhittithaṃbhasopānaṃ[^010-14] nānāvi-
dhamālākammalatākammavicittaṃabhibhavantamivarā-
jabhavanavibhūtiṃ avahasantam[^010-15] iva devavimānasiriṃ si-
riyā niketam iva ekanipātanatittham[^010-16] iva ca devamanussa-
nayanavibhaṅgānaṃ[^010-17] lokarāmaṇeyyakam iva[^010-18] sampiṇḍitaṃ
daṭṭhabbasāramaṇḍaṃ maṇḍapaṃ[^010-19] kārāpetvā, vividhaku-

     tasmiṃ[^011-5] kho pana samaye[^011-6] ekacce bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ
Ānandaṃ sandhāya evam āhaṃsu: imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṃghe
eko bhikkhu vissagandhaṃ vāyanto[^011-7] vicaratīti.
     thero taṃ sutvā imasmiṃ bhikkhusaṃghe añño vissagan-
dhaṃ vāyanto vicaraṇabhikkhu nāma n' atthi, addhā ete maṃ
sandhāya vadantīti saṃvegaṃ āpajji. ekacce bhikkhū āyas-
mantaṃ Ānandaṃ āhaṃsu[^011-8]: sve āvuso sannipāto[^011-9] tvañ ca

caṃkamena bahi vītināmetvā visesaṃ nibbattetum asak-

accāraddhaṃ viriyan,[^012-5] tena me cittaṃ uddhaccāya saṃ-
vattati. handāhaṃ viriyasamathaṃ[^012-6] yojemīti, caṃkamā
orohitvā pādadhovanaṭṭhāne[^012-7] ṭhatvā pāde dhovitvā vihāraṃ
pavisitvā[^012-8] mañcake nisīditvā thokaṃ vissamissāmīti kāyaṃ
mañcake apanāmesi.[^012-9] dve pādā bhūmito muttā sīsam bim-
bohanam asampattaṃ.[^012-10] etasmim antare anupādāya āsavehi

hattaṃ ahosi.[^012-13] tena imasmiṃ sāsane anipanno anisinno[^012-14]

thero cintesi: idāni mayhaṃ hamanakālo ti. tato attano

ānubhāvaṃ dassento paṭhaviyaṃ[^013-1] nimujjitvā attano āsane

atha kho āyasmā Mahākassapo āyasmantaṃ Upāliṃ paṭha-
massa pārājikassa vatthum pi pucchi, nidānam pi pucchi,
puggalam pi pucchi, paññattim pi pucchi, anuppaññattim[^014-4]
pi pucchi, āpattim pi pucchi, anāpattim pi pucchi. yathā[^014-5]
ca paṭhamassa tathā dutiyassa tathā tatiyassa tathā catut-
thassa pārājikassa vatthum pi pucchi --pe-- anāpattim pi
pucchi,[^014-5] puṭṭho puṭṭho Upālitthero[^014-6] vissajjesi.
tato[^014-7] imāni cattāri pārājikāni pārājikakaṇḍaṃ nāma idan
ti saṃgahaṃ āropetvā[^014-8] terasa saṃghādisesāni terasakan ti
ṭhapesuṃ.[^014-9] dve sikkhāpadāni aniyatānīti ṭhapesuṃ tiṃ-
sasikkhāpadāni nissaggiyapācittiyānīti ṭhapesuṃ. dvena-
vutisikkhāpadāni pācittiyānīti ṭhapesuṃ. catāri sikkhā-
padāni pāṭidesaniyānīti ṭhapesuṃ. pañcasattatisikkhāpadāni

Rājagahaṃ antarā ca Nālandaṃ[^016-1] rājāgārake Ambalaṭṭhikāyan
ti. kaṃ ārabbhā 'ti. Suppiyañ ca paribbājakaṃ Brahma-
dattañ ca māṇavakan[^016-2] ti. [^016-3]kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ. vaṇṇā-
vaṇṇe ti.[^016-3]
atha kho āyasmā Mahākassapo āyamantaṃ Ānandaṃ
Brahmajālassa nidānam pi pucchi, puggalam pi pucchi.
Sāmaññaphalaṃ panāvuso Ānanda kattha bhāsitan ti.
Rājagahe bhante Jīvakambavane ti. kena saddhin ti.
Ajātasattunā Vedehiputtena saddhin ti. atha kho āyasmā
Mahākassapo āyasmantaṃ Ānandaṃ Sāmaññaphalassa ni-
dānam pi pucchi, puggalam pi pucchi. eten' eva upāyena
pañca pi[^016-4] nikāye pucchi. pañca nikāyā nāma Dīghanikāyo
Majjhimanikāyo Saṃyuttanikāyo Aṅguttaranikāyo Khud-
dakanikāyo ti. tattha Khuddakanikāyo nāma cattāro ni-
kāye ṭhapetvā avasesaṃ buddhavacanaṃ.
tattha vinayo āyasmatā Upālittherena[^016-5] vissajjito,[^016-6] sesa-
Khuddakanikāyo[^016-7] cattāro ca nikāyā Ānandattherena.[^016-8]
tad' etaṃ[^016-9] sabbam pi[^016-10] buddhavacanaṃ rasavasena eka-
vidhaṃ, dhammavinayavasena duvidhaṃ,[^016-11] paṭhamamajjhi-
mapacchimavasena[^016-12] tividhaṃ tathā piṭakavasena, nikāyava-
sena pañcavidhaṃ, aṅgavasena navavidhaṃ, dhammak-
khandhavasena caturāsītisahassavidhan ti veditabbaṃ.
kathaṃ rasavasena ekavidhaṃ. yaṃ hi bhagavatā anut-
taraṃ sammāsambodhiṃ abhisambujjhitvā yāva anupādise-
sāya nibbānadhātuyā parinibbāyati[^016-13] etthantare pañcacattā-
ḷīsavassāni[^016-14] devamanussanāgayakkhādayo anusāsantena[^016-15]
paccavekkhantena vā vuttaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ ekarasaṃ vimut-
tirasam eva hoti. evaṃ rasavasena ekavidhaṃ.

ayaṃ hi abhisaddo vuddhisalakkhaṇapūjitaparicchinnādhi-
kesu dissati. tathā h' esa, bāḷhā[^020-2] me[^020-3] dukkhā vedanā abhik-
kamanti no paṭikkamantīti[^020-4] ādisu[^020-5] vuddhiyaṃ[^020-6] āgato. yā
tā rattiyo abhiññātā abhilakkhitā ti ādisu salakkhaṇe.[^020-7]
rājābhirājā manujindo[^020-8] ti ādisu pūjite. paṭibalo vinetuṃ
abhidhamme abhivinaye ti ādisu paricchinne. aññamaññaṃ[^020-9]
saṃkaravirahite dhamme ca vinaye cā 'ti vuttaṃ hoti.
abhikkantena vaṇṇenā 'ti ādisu adhike. ettha ca rūpūpapat-
tiyā maggaṃ bhāveti, mettāsahagatena cetasā ekaṃ disaṃ
pharitvā viharatīti ādinā nayena vuddhimanto pi dhammā
vuttā. rūpārammaṇaṃ vā saddārammaṇaṃ vā ti ādinā
nayena ārammaṇādīhi lakkhaṇīyattā[^020-10] salakkhaṇā pi. sekhā
dhammā asekhā dhammā lokuttarā dhammā ti ādinā nayena
pūjitā pi pūjārahā[^020-11] ti adhippāyo. phasso hoti, vedanā ho-
tīti ādinā nayena sabhāvaparicchinnattā paricchinnā pi.[^020-12]
mahaggatā dhammā appamāṇā dhammā anuttarā dhammā
ti ādinā nayena adhikā pi dhammā vuttā.
ten' etam etassa vacanatthakosallatthaṃ vuttaṃ:
     yaṃ ettha vuddhimanto salakkhaṇā pūjitā paricchinnā[^020-13]

     piṭakaṃ piṭakatthavidū pariyattibhājanatthato[^020-15] āhu,

tena evaṃ duvidhatthena[^021-4] piṭakasaddena saha samāsaṃ
katvā vinayo ca so piṭakañ ca pariyattibhāvato tassa tassa[^021-5]
atthassa bhājanato cā 'ti Vinayapiṭakaṃ. Yathā vutten' eva
nayena suttañ ca[^021-6] taṃ piṭakaṃ cā 'ti Suttapiṭakaṃ,[^021-7] abhi-
dhammo ca so piṭakaṃ cā 'ti Abhidhammapiṭakan ti, evam
ete tayo pi vinayādayo ñeyyā. evaṃ ñatvā ca[^021-8] puna pi tesv
eva[^021-9] piṭakesu nānappakārakosallatthaṃ:
     desanāsāsanakathābhedaṃ tesu[^021-10] yathārahaṃ
     sikkhāppahānagambhīrabhāvañ[^021-11] ca paridīpaye.
     pariyattibhedaṃ sampattiṃ vipattiñ[^021-12] cāpi yaṃ yahiṃ[^021-13]
     pāpuṇāti yathā bhikkhu tam pi sabbaṃ vibhāvaye ti.[^021-14]
tatrāyaṃ paridīpanā vibhāvanā ca. etāni hi tīṇi[^021-15] piṭakāni
yathāk-kamam āṇāvohāraparamatthadesanā,[^021-16] yathāparādha-
yathānulomayathādhammasāsanāni, saṃvarāsaṃvaradiṭṭhi-
viniveṭhananāmarūpaparicchedakathā[^021-17] cā'ti[^021-18] vuccanti. ettha
hi Vinayapiṭakaṃ āṇārahena bhagavatā āṇābāhullato desi-
tattā āṇādesanā, Suttapiṭakaṃ[^021-19] vohārakusalena bhagavatā
vohārabāhullato desitattā vohāradesanā, Abhidhammapiṭa-
kaṃ[^021-20] paramatthakusalena bhagavatā paramatthabāhullato
desitattā paramatthadesanā ti vuccati.

tathā paṭhamaṃ ye te pacurāparādhā sattā te yathāparā-
dhaṃ ettha sāsitā ti yathāparādhasāsanaṃ. dutiyaṃ anekaj-
jhāsayānusayacaritādhimuttikā[^022-1] sattā yathānulomaṃ ettha
sāsitā ti yathānulomasāsanaṃ. tatiyaṃ dhammapuñja-
matte ahaṃ mamā 'ti saññino sattā yathādhammaṃ ettha
sāsitā ti yathādhammasāsanan ti vuccati.
tathā paṭhamaṃ ajjhācārapaṭipakkhabhūto saṃvarāsaṃ-
varo ettha kathito ti saṃvarāsaṃvarakathā.[^022-2] dutiyaṃ dvā-
saṭṭhi diṭṭhipaṭipakkhabhūtā diṭṭhiviniveṭhanā[^022-3] ettha ka-
thitā ti diṭṭhiviniveṭhanakathā.[^022-3]
tatiyaṃ rāgādipaṭipakkhabhūto nāmarūpaparicchedo ettha
kathito ti nāmarūpaparicchedakathā ti vuccati.
tīsu pi ca etesu tisso[^022-4] sikkhā tīni[^022-5] pahāṇāni catubbidho ca
gambhīrabhāvo veditabbo. tathā hi Vinayapiṭake visesena
adhisīlasikkhā vuttā, Suttapiṭake adhicittasikkhā, Abhidham-
mapiṭake adhipaññāsikkhā. Vinayapiṭake ca vītikkamap-
pahānaṃ[^022-6] kilesānaṃ vītikkamapaṭipakkhattā sīlassa. Sut-
tapiṭake pariyuṭṭhānappahānaṃ pariyuṭṭhānapaṭipakkhattā
samādhissa. Abhidhammapiṭake anusayappahānaṃ anu-
sayapaṭipakkhattā paññāya. paṭhame ca tadaṅgappahānaṃ
kilesānaṃ itaresu vikkhambhanasamucchedappahānāni. pa-
ṭhame[^022-7] duccaritasaṃkilesassa pahānaṃ itaresu taṇhādiṭṭhi-
saṃkilesānaṃ. ekamekasmiṃ c' ettha catubbidho pi dham-
matthadesanāpaṭivedhagambhīrabhāvo veditabbo. tattha
dhammo ti pāli,[^022-8] attho ti tassā yeva attho,[^022-9] desanā ti tassā
manasā avatthāpitāya[^022-10] pāliyā[^022-11] desanā, paṭivedho ti pāliyā
pāliatthassa[^022-12] ca yathābhūtāvabodho. tīsu pi c' etesu ete

dhammatthadesanā paṭivedhā yasmā sasādīhi viya mahā-
samuddo mandabuddhīhi[^023-1] dukkhogāhā[^023-2] alabbhaneyyapatiṭ-
ṭhā ca, tasmā gambhīrā. evaṃ ekamekasmiṃ ettha[^023-3] catub-
bidho pi gambhīrabhāvo veditabbo.
aparo nayo. dhammo ti hetu, vuttaṃ h' etaṃ: hetumhi
ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā ti.[^023-4] attho ti hetuphalaṃ, vut-
taṃ h' etaṃ: hetuphale ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā ti. desanā
ti paññatti. yathādhammaṃ dhammābhilāpo ti adhippāyo.[^023-5]
paṭivedho ti abhisamayo, so ca lokiyo lokuttaro[^023-6] visayato[^023-7]
asammohato ca atthānurūpaṃ dhammesu dhammānurūpaṃ
atthesu paññatti, pathānurūpaṃ[^023-8] paññattīsu[^023-9] avabodho.[^023-10]
idāni yasmā etesu[^023-11] piṭakesu yaṃ yaṃ dhammajātaṃ attha-
jātaṃ yo cāyaṃ[^023-12] yathā yathā ñāpetabbo attho sotūnaṃ[^023-13]
ñāṇassa abhimukho hoti tathā tathā tadatthajotakadesanā[^023-14]
yo c' ettha aviparītāvabodhasaṃkhāto[^023-15] paṭivedho[^023-16] sabbaṃ

ca, tasmā[^023-21] evam pi ekamekasmiṃ ettha[^023-22] catubbidho pi
gambhīrabhāvo[^023-23] veditabbo.

ettāvatā ca:
     desanāsāsanakathābhedaṃ[^024-1] tesu yathārahaṃ
     sikkhāppahānagambhīrabhāvañ[^024-2] ca paridīpaye
iti ayaṃ gāthā vuttatthā[^024-3] hoti.
     pariyattibhedaṃ sampattiṃ vippattiṃ cāpi yaṃ yahiṃ
     pāpuṇāti yathā bhikkhu tam pi sabbaṃ vibhāvaye iti.
ettha pana tīsu piṭakesu tividho[^024-4] pariyattibhedo daṭṭhabbo.
tisso hi pariyattiyo, alagaddūpamā nissaraṇatthā bhaṇḍā-
gārikapariyattīti. tattha[^024-5] duggahitā[^024-6] upārambhādihetu pari-
yāputā alagaddūpamā.[^024-7] yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ:
seyyathā pi bhikkhave puriso alagaddatthiko alagadda-
gavesī alagaddapariyesanaṃ caramāno so passeyya mahan-
taṃ alagaddaṃ. tam enaṃ[^024-8] bhoge vā naṅguṭṭhe vā gaṇ-
heyya. tassa so alagaddo paṭiparivattitvā[^024-9] hatthe vā bāhāya
vā aññatarasmiṃ vā aṅgapaccaṅge ḍaseyya.[^024-10] so tatonidā-
naṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigaccheyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ.
taṃ kissa hetu. duggahītattā[^024-11] bhikkhave alagaddassa.
evam eva kho bhikkhave idh' ekacce moghapurisā dhammaṃ
pariyāpuṇanti suttaṃ --pe-- Vedallaṃ.[^024-12] te taṃ dhammaṃ
pariyāpuṇitvā [^024-13]tesaṃ dhammānaṃ paññāya atth na upa-
parikkhanti,[^024-13] tesaṃ te dhammā paññāya atthaṃ anupaparik-
khataṃ na nijjhānaṃ khamanti, te pārambhānisamsā[^024-14]
c' eva dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti itivādappamokkhānisaṃsā[^024-15]
ca,[^024-16] yassa c' atthāya dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti, taṃ c' assa
atthaṃ nānubhonti, tesaṃ te dhammā duggahītā dīgharattaṃ
ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti. taṃ kissa hetu. duggahī-
tattā[^024-17] bhikkhave dhammānan ti.[^024-18]

<!-- PTS Page 025 -->

##### *[PTS Page 025]*

yā pana suggahītā[^025-1] sīlakkhadhādipāripūriṃ[^025-2] yeva ākaṃ-
khamānena pariyāputā[^025-3] na upārambhādihetu ayaṃ nissara-
ṇatthā.
yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ:
tesaṃ te dhammā suggahītā[^025-1] dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya
saṃvattanti.
taṃ kissa hetu. suggahītattā bhikkhave dhammānan ti.
yaṃ[^025-4] pana pariññātakkhandho pahīṇakileso[^025-5] bhāvitamaggo
paṭividdhākuppo[^025-6] sacchikatanirodho khīṇāsavo kevalaṃ pa-
veṇipālanatthāya[^025-7] vaṃsānurakkhaṇatthāya pariyāpuṇāti,[^025-8]
ayaṃ bhaṇḍāgārikapariyattīti.
vinaye pana[^025-9] suppaṭipanno bhikkhū[^025-10] sīlasampattim[^025-11] nis-
sāya tisso vijjā pāpuṇāti, tāsaṃ yeva ca tattha pabhedavaca-
nato.
sutte suppaṭipanno samādhisampadaṃ nissāya cha abhiññā
pāpuṇāti, tāsaṃ yeva ca tattha pabhedavacanato.
abhidhamme suppaṭipanno paññāsampadaṃ nissāya ca-
tasso paṭisambhidā pāpuṇāti, tāsañ[^025-12] ca tatth' eva pabhe-
davacanato.
evam etesu suppaṭipanno yathākkamaṃ[^025-13] imaṃ vijjātta-
yaṃ[^025-14] chaḷabhiññācatupaṭisambhidābhedaṃ[^025-15] sampattiṃ pā-
puṇāti.
vinaye pana duppaṭipanno anuññātasukhasamphassāttha-
raṇapāpuraṇādiphassasāmaññato[^025-16] paṭikkhittesu upādinna-
phassādisu[^025-17] anavajjasaññī hoti. vuttam pi h' etaṃ[^025-18]:
tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ desitaṃ ājānāmi yathā
ye me antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā te paṭisevato
nālaṃ antarāyāyā 'ti. tato dussīlabhāvaṃ pāpuṇāti.

Yaso ca Sānasambhūto[^035-1] ete saddhivihārikā
     therā Ānandattherassa diṭṭhapubbā tathāgataṃ.
     Sumano[^035-2] Vāsabhagāmī ca ñeyyā[^035-3] saddhivihārikā
     dve ime Anuruddhassa diṭṭhapubbā tathāgataṃ.
     dutiyo pana saṃgīto yehi therehi saṃgaho
     sabbe pi pannabhārā te katakiccā anāsavā ti,
ayaṃ dutiyasaṃgīti.
evam imaṃ[^035-4] dutiyasaṃgītiṃ saṃgāyitvā te[^035-5] therā
uppajjissati nu kho anāgate pi sāsanassa evarūpaṃ abbudan
ti olokayamānā[^035-6] idaṃ[^035-7] addasaṃsu: ito vassasatassa upari
aṭṭhārasame vasse Pāṭaliputte[^035-8] Dhammāsoko nāma rājā
uppajjitvā sakala-Jambudīpe rajjaṃ kāressatīti.[^035-9] so bud-

sati. tato titthiyā lābhasakkāraṃ patthayamānā sāsane pab-
bajitvā sakaṃ sakaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paridīpessanti. evaṃ sāsane
mahantaṃ abbudaṃ uppajjissatīti.
atha nesaṃ etad ahosi: kin nu kho mayaṃ etasmiṃ abbude
uppanne sammukhā bhavissāma na bhavissāmā 'ti. atha[^035-10]

disvāna nesaṃ[^035-17] etad ahosi: sace mayaṃ etassa brahmuno

esa Moggalibrāhmaṇassa[^036-1] gehe paṭisandhiṃ gahessati tato
ca mantehi palobhito nikkhamitvā pabbajissati, so evaṃ
pabbajitvā sakalabuddhavacanaṃ[^036-2] uggahetvā adhigatapaṭi-
sambhido hutvā titthiye madditvā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vinic-
chinitvā[^036-3] sāsanaṃ paggaṇhissatīti.
te[^036-4] Brahmalokaṃ gantvā Tissaṃ mahābrahmānaṃ etad
avocuṃ: ito vassasatassa upari aṭṭhārasame vasse sāsane
mahantaṃ abbudaṃ uppajjissati, mayañ ca[^036-5] sakalaṃ ma-
nussalokam chakāmāvacaradevalokañ[^036-6] ca olokayamānā kañci[^036-7]
sāsanaṃ paggaṇhetuṃ[^036-8] samatthaṃ adisvā Brahmalokaṃ
vicinantā bhavantaṃ[^036-9] addasāma. sādhu sappurisa manus-
saloke nibbattitvā dasabalassa sāsanaṃ paggaṇhituṃ paṭiñ-
ñaṃ dehīti.[^036-10] evaṃ vutte mahābrahmā, ahaṃ kira sāsane
uppannaṃ abbudaṃ sodhetvā sāsanaṃ paggahetuṃ samattho
bhavissāmīti haṭṭhapahaṭṭho udaggudaggo hutvā sādhū 'ti
paṭisuṇitvā paṭiññaṃ adāsi. therā Brahmaloke taṃ karaṇī-
yaṃ tīretvā puna paccāgamiṃsu.
tena kho pana samayena Siggavatthero ca Candavajjit-
thero[^036-11] ca dve pi navakā honti daharabhikkhū tipiṭakadharā
pattapaṭisambhidā khīṇāsavā, te taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ na pāpu-
ṇiṃsu.[^036-12] therā tumhe āvuso amhākaṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇe
no sahāyakā ahuvattha,[^036-13] tena vo idaṃ daṇḍakammaṃ hotu:
Tisso nāma brahmā[^036-14] Moggalibrāhmaṇassa gehe paṭisandhiṃ
gaṇhissati, taṃ tumhākaṃ eko nīharitvā pabbājetu, eko
buddhavacanaṃ uggaṇhāpetū 'ti vatvā sabbe pi yāvatāyu-
kaṃ ṭhatvā.
     Sabbakāmippabhutayo te pi therā mahiddhikā[^036-15]
     aggikkhandhā va lokamhi[^036-16] jalitvā parinibbutā.[^036-17]
     dutiyasaṃgahaṃ[^036-18] katvā visodhetvāna sāsanam

anāgate pi katvāna hetuṃ saddhammasuddhiyā
     khīṇāsavā vasippattā pabhinnapaṭisambhidā
     aniccatāvasaṃ therā te pi nāma upāgatā.
     evaṃ aniccataṃ jammiṃ ñatvā durabhisambhavaṃ
     taṃ pattuṃ vāyame dhīro yaṃ niccaṃ amataṃ padanti.
ettāvatā sabbākārena dutiyasaṃgītivaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā hoti.
Tisso pi kho mahābrahmā Brahmalokā[^037-1] cavitvā Moggali-
brāhmaṇassa gehe paṭisandhiṃ aggahesi. Siggavatthero pi
tassa paṭisandhigahaṇato[^037-2] pabhuti[^037-3] sattavassāni brahma-
ṇassa gehaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi. ekadivasam pi uḷuṃkamattaṃ
vā yāguṃ kaṭacchumattaṃ vā bhattaṃ nālattha.[^037-4] sattan-
naṃ pana vassānaṃ accayena ekadivasaṃ, aticchatha bhante
ti vacanamattaṃ alattha.[^037-5] taṃ divasam eva brāhmaṇo pi
bahiddhā kiñci karaṇīyaṃ katvā āgacchanto paṭipathe
theraṃ disvā, bho pabbajita amhākaṃ gehaṃ agamitthā 'ti[^037-6]
āha. āma brāhma agamamhā 'ti.[^037-7] api kiñci labhitthā
'ti. āma brāhmaṇa labhimhā 'ti. so gehaṃ gantvā pucchi:
tassa pabbajitassa kiñci adatthā 'ti. na kiñci adamhā 'ti.
brāhmaṇo dutiyadivase gharadvāre yeva nisīdi, ajja pabba-
jitaṃ musāvādena niggahessāmīti. thero[^037-8] dutiyadivase brāh-
maṇassa gharadvāraṃ[^037-9] saṃpatto.[^037-10] brāhmaṇo theraṃ disvā
'va evaṃ āha: tumhe hiyyo[^037-11] amhākaṃ gehe kiñci aladdhā
yeva labhimhā 'ti avocuttha. vaṭṭati nu kho tumhākaṃ
musāvādo ti. thero āha: mayaṃ brāhmaṇa tumhākaṃ gehe
sattavassāni aticchathā 'ti vacanamattam pi alabhitvā
hiyyo[^037-11] aticchathā 'ti vacanamattaṃ labhimha. ath' etaṃ
paṭisanthāraṃ[^037-12] upādāya evam avocamhā 'ti.
brāhmaṇo cintesi: ime paṭisanthāramattam[^037-13] pi labhitvā
labhimhā 'ti pasaṃsanti. aññaṃ kiñci khādaniyaṃ bhoja-

niyaṃ[^038-1] labhitvā kasmā na ppasaṃsantīti pasīditvā attano
atthāya paṭiyāditabhattato kaṭacchubhikkhaṃ[^038-2] tadūpiyañ[^038-3]
ca vyañjanaṃ dāpetvā imaṃ bhikkhaṃ sabbakālaṃ tumhe
labhissathā 'ti āha. so punadivasato pabhuti upasaṃkaman-
tassa therassa upasamaṃ disvā[^038-4] bhiyyosomattāya pasīditvā
theraṃ niccakālaṃ attano[^038-5] ghare bhattavissaggakaraṇat-
thāya yāci. thero adhivāsetvā divase divase bhattakiccaṃ
katvā gacchanto thokathokaṃ[^038-6] buddhavacanaṃ kathetvā
gacchati.
so pi kho māṇavako soḷasavassuddesiko yeva tiṇṇaṃ vedā-
naṃ pāragu[^038-7] ahosi. Brahmalokato āgatasuddhasattassa
āsane vā sayane vā añño koci nisajjitā vā nipajjitā vā[^038-8] n'
atthi. so yadā ācariyagharaṃ gacchati tadāssa[^038-9] mañcapī-
ṭhaṃ setena vatthena paṭicchādetvā laggetvā ṭhapenti.
thero cintesi: samayo dāni māṇavakaṃ pabbājetuṃ,
cirañ ca me idhāgacchantassa[^038-10] na kāci[^038-11] māṇavakena saddhiṃ
kathā uppajjati.[^038-12] handa dāni iminā upāyena pallaṅkaṃ
nissāya[^038-13] uppajjissatīti. gehaṃ gantvā yathā tasmiṃ gehe
ṭhapetvā māṇavakassa pallaṅkaṃ aññaṃ na kiñci āsanaṃ
dissati tathā adhiṭṭhāsi.
brāhmaṇassa gehe jano theraṃ disvā aññaṃ kiñci āsanaṃ
apassanto māṇavakassa pallaṅkaṃ attharitvā[^038-14] adāsi. nisīdi
thero pallaṅke. māṇavako pi kho taṃkhaṇaṃ yeva[^038-15] ācari-

kassa caṇḍikkabhāve evaṃ āha[^038-18]: kiṃ pana tvaṃ māṇavaka

kañci[^039-1] mantaṃ jānāsīti. māṇavo[^039-2], bho pabbajita mayi

atha kho māṇavo[^039-4] tīsu vedesu sanighaṇḍukeṭubhesu[^039-5]

ṭhānāni[^039-7] yesaṃ[^039-8] nayaṃ n' eva attanā passati nāpi 'ssa[^039-9] ācariyo
addasa tesu[^039-10] theraṃ pucchi.
thero pakatiyāpi tiṇṇaṃ vedānaṃ pāragu,[^039-11] idāni pana
patisambhidapatto,[^039-12] ten' assa n' atthi nesaṃ[^039-13] pañhānaṃ vis-
sajjane 'va bhāro[^039-14] ti tāvad eva te[^039-15] pañhe vissajjetvā[^039-16] māṇa-
vaṃ[^039-17] āha: māṇava[^039-18] ahaṃ tayā bahuṃ pucchito, aham pi
dāni taṃ ekaṃ pañhaṃ pucchāmi, vyākarissasi me ti. āma
bho pabbajita puccha, vyākarissāmīti.
thero Cittayamake imaṃ pañhaṃ pucchi: yassa cittaṃ
uppajjati na nirujjhati, tassa cittaṃ nirujjhissati n' uppajjis-
sati,[^039-19] yassa vā pana cittaṃ[^039-20] nirujjhissati n' uppajjissati,[^039-19]
tassa cittaṃ uppajjati na nirujjhatīti. māṇavo uddhaṃ vā
adho vā sarituṃ[^039-21] asakkonto, kiṃ nāma bho pabbajita idan ti
āha. buddhamanto nāmāyaṃ māṇavā 'ti. sakkā panāyāṃ
bho mayhaṃ pi dātun ti. sakkā māṇava amhehi gahitaṃ[^039-22]
pabbajjaṃ gaṇhantassa dātun ti.

##### *[PTS Page 040]*

<i>40                   Samantapāsādikā
</i>     tato māṇavo mātāpitaro upasaṃkamitvā āha: ayaṃ pabba-
jito buddhamantaṃ nāma jānāti na ca attano santike apabba-
jitassa deti. ahaṃ etassa santike pabbajitvā mantaṃ uggaṇ-
hissāmīti. ath' assa mātāpitaro, pabbajitvā pi no putto
mante gaṇhātu,[^040-1] gahetvā punāgamissatīti[^040-2] maññamānā,
gaṇha[^040-3] puttā 'ti anujāniṃsu.
thero dārakaṃ pabbājetvā dvatiṃsākārakammaṭṭhānaṃ
tāva ācikkhi. so tattha parikammaṃ karonto na cirass' eva[^040-4]
sotāpattiphale patiṭṭhāsi.[^040-5] tato thero cintesi: sāmaṇero
sotāpattiphale patiṭṭhito, abhabbo dāni sāsanato nivattituṃ.
sace pan' assāhaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ vaḍḍhetvā katheyyaṃ,
arahattaṃ pāpuṇeyya[^040-6] appossukko[^040-7] bhaveyya buddhava-
canaṃ gahetuṃ. samayo dāni naṃ Candavajjittherassa[^040-8]
santikaṃ pesetun ti.
tato naṃ[^040-9] āha: ehi tvaṃ sāmaṇera therassa santikaṃ
gantvā buddhavacanaṃ uggaṇha. mama vacanena[^040-10] āro-
gyaṃ puccha evaṃ ca vadehi: upajjhāyo maṃ bhante
tumhākam santikaṃ pahiṇīti.
konāmo te upajjhāyo ti ca vutte Siggavatthero nāma
bhante ti vadeyyāsi.
ahaṃ konāmo ti vutte avaṃ vadeyyāsi: mama upajjhāyo
bhante tumhākaṃ nāmaṃ jānātīti. evaṃ bhante ti kho
Tisso sāmaṇero theraṃ abhivādetvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā
anupubbena Candavajjittherassa[^040-8] santikaṃ gantvā vanditvā
ekamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi.
thero sāmaṇeraṃ pucchi[^040-11]: kuto āgato 'sītī āha. upajjhāyo
maṃ bhante tumhākaṃ santikaṃ pahiṇīti. konāmo te
upajjhāyo ti. Siggavatthero nāma bhante ti. ahaṃ konāmo
ti. mama upajjhāyo bhante tumhākaṃ nāmaṃ jānātīti.
pattacīvaraṃ dāni paṭisāmehīti. sādhu bhante ti sāmaṇero

pattacīvaraṃ paṭisāmetvā punadivase pariveṇaṃ sammaj-
jitvā udakadantapoṇaṃ upaṭṭhāpesi.[^041-1]
thero[^041-2] sammaṭṭhaṭṭhānaṃ[^041-3] pana sammajji, taṃ udakaṃ
chaḍḍetvā aññaṃ udakaṃ āhari, tañ ca dantakaṭṭhaṃ apa-
netvā aññaṃ dantakaṭṭhaṃ gaṇhi. evaṃ sattadivasāni
katvā sattame divase puna pucchi.
sāmaṇero puna pi pubbe kathitasadisam eva kathesi.
thero so vatāyaṃ brāhmaṇo[^041-4] ti sañjānitvā, kimatthaṃ āgato
'sīti āha. buddhavacanaṃ uggaṇhanatthāya[^041-5] bhante ti.
thero uggaṇha dāni sāmaṇerā 'ti[^041-6] vatvā punadivasato pabhuti.[^041-7]
buddhavacanaṃ paṭṭhapesi. Tisso sāmaṇero 'va hutvā
ṭhapetvā Vinayapiṭakaṃ sabbaṃ buddhavacanaṃ uggaṇhi
saddhiṃ aṭṭhakathāya. upasampannakāle pana avassiko
'va samāno[^041-8] tipiṭakadharo ahosi.
ācariyupajjhāyā[^041-9] Moggaliputtatissattherassa hatthe saka-

parinibbāyiṃsu.

naṃ vaḍḍhetvā arahattaṃ patto[^041-12] bahunnaṃ[^041-13] dhammavi-
nayaṃ vācesi.
tena kho pana samayena Bindusārassa rañño ekasataputtā
ahesuṃ. te sabbe Asoko attanā saddhiṃ ekamātikaṃ

Chaddantadahe ca[^043-1] uṭṭhitassa sālino nava vāhasahassāni
divase divase suvā[^043-2] āharanti, musikā[^043-3] nitthusakaṃ[^043-4] karonti,
eko pi khaṇḍataṇḍulo na hoti,[^043-5] rañño sabbaṭṭhānesu ayaṃ
eva taṇḍulo paribhogaṃ gacchati. madhumakkhikā madhuṃ
karonti. kammārasālādīsu[^043-6] acchā kūṭaṃ paharanti. kara-
vikasakuṇā[^043-7] āgantvā madhurassaraṃ vikūjantā[^043-8] rañño bali-
kammaṃ karonti.
imāhi iddhīhi samannāgato[^043-9] rājā ekadivasaṃ suvaṇṇa-
saṃkhalikabandhanaṃ pesetvā catunnaṃ buddhānaṃ adhi-
gatarūpadassanaṃ kappāyukaṃ Kālaṃ[^043-10] nāma[^043-11] nāgarājāṃ
ānayitvā setacchattassa heṭṭhā mahārahe pallaṇke nisīdā-
petvā anekasatavaṇṇehi jalajathalajapupphehi suvaṇṇa-
pupphehi ca pūjaṃ katvā sabbālaṅkārapatimaṇḍitehi[^043-12]

tañāṇassa tāva me saddhammavaracakkavattino sammā-
sambuddhassa rūpaṃ imesaṃ akkhīnaṃ āpāthaṃ[^043-15] karohīti
vatvā tena nimmitaṃ sakalasarīre[^043-16] vippakiṇṇapuññappa-

visadavisphuritasobhāsamujjalam[^043-22] iva gaganatalaṃ nī-

parikkhepavilāsitāya[^044-1] sandhyāppabhānurāgaindadhanuvij-
jullatāparikkhittam[^044-2] iva kanakagirisikharaṃ nānāvirāgavi-
malaketumālāsamujjalitacārumatthakasobhaṃ[^044-3]. nayanara-
sāyanam[^044-4] iva brahmadevamanujanāgayakkhagaṇānaṃ bud-
dharūpam passanto sattadivasāni akkhipūjaṃ nāma akāsi.
rājā kira abhisekaṃ pāpuṇitvā tīṇi yeva saṃvaccharāni
bāhirakapāsaṇḍaṃ parigaṇhi, catutthe saṃvacchare buddha-
sāsane pasīdi. tassa kira pitā Bindusāro brāhmaṇabhatto
ahosi. so brāhmaṇānañ ca brāhmaṇajātiyapāsaṇḍānañ ca
paṇḍaraṅgaparibbājakādīnaṃ saṭṭhisahassamattānaṃ nicca-
bhattaṃ paṭṭhapesi.
Asoko[^044-5] pitarā pavattitaṃ dānaṃ attano antepure tath' eva
dadamāno ekadivasaṃ sīhapañjare[^044-6] ṭhito te upasamapari-
bāhirena ācārena bhuñjamāne asantindriye[^044-7] avinītairiyā-
pathe disvā cintesi: īdisaṃ dānaṃ[^044-8] upaparikkhitvā yuttaṭ-
ṭhāne dātum vaṭṭatīti. evaṃ cintetvā amacce āha: gaccha-
tha[^044-9] bhaṇe[^044-10] attano attano sādhusammate[^044-11] samaṇabrāh-
maṇe[^044-12] antepuraṃ atiharatha[^044-13] dānaṃ dassāmā 'ti. amaccā,
sādhu devā 'ti rañño paṭissutvā[^044-14] te te paṇḍaraṅgaparibbāja-
kājīvikaniganthādayo[^044-15] ānetvā, ime mahārāja amhākaṃ ara-
hanto ti āhaṃsu.[^044-16]
atha rājā antepure uccāvacāni āsanāni paññāpetvā[^044-17] āgac-
chantū 'ti vatvā āgatāgate āha: attano attano patirūpe[^044-18] āsane

nisīdathā 'ti. te[^045-1] ekacce bhaddapīṭhakesu ekacce phalaka-

sāro ti ñatvā tesaṃ anurūpaṃ khādaniyabhojaniyaṃ[^045-4] datvā
uyyojesi. evaṃ gacchante kāle ekadivasaṃ[^045-5] sīhapañjare[^045-6]
ṭhito addasa Nigrodhaṃ[^045-7] sāmaṇeraṃ rājaṅgaṇena gacchantaṃ
dantaṃ guttaṃ santindriyaṃ[^045-8] iriyāpathasampannaṃ.
ko panāyaṃ Nigrodho nāma. Bindusārarañño jeṭṭhaput-
tassa Sumanarājakumārassa[^045-9] putto.
tatrāyaṃ anupubbikathā[^045-10]: Bindusārarañño kira dubbala-
kāle yeva Asokakumāro attanā laddhaṃ Ujjenirajjaṃ pahāya
āgantvā[^045-11] sabbaṃ nagaraṃ[^045-12] attano hatthagataṃ katvā Su-
manaṃ rājakumāraṃ[^045-13] aggahesi. taṃ divasam eva Suma-
nassa rājakumārassa Sumanā nāma rājadevī[^045-14] paripuṇṇa-
gabbhā ahosi. sā aññātakavesena nikkhamitvā avidūre
aññataraṃ caṇḍālagāmaṃ sandhāya gacchantī jeṭṭhaka-
caṇḍālassa gehato avidūre aññatarasmim[^045-15] nigrodharukkhe
adhivatthāya devatāya, ito ehi Sumaneti[^045-16] vadantiyā saddaṃ
sutvā tassā samīpaṃ gatā.[^045-17]
devatā attano ānubhāvena ekaṃ sālaṃ[^045-18] nimminitvā, ettha
vasāhīti pādāsi.[^045-19] sā taṃ sālaṃ pāvisi. gatadivase yeva[^045-20]
puttaṃ vijāyi. sā tassa nigrodhadevatāya pariggahitattā
Nigrodho tv eva nāmaṃ akāsi. jeṭṭhakacaṇḍālo diṭṭhadi-
vasato pabhuti taṃ attano sāmidhītaraṃ[^045-21] maññamāno ni-
baddhavattaṃ[^045-22] paṭṭhapesi. rājadhītā tattha satta vassāni
vasi.

Nigrodhakumāro pi satta vassiko jāto. tadā Mahāvaru-
ṇatthero nāma eko arahā dārakassa hetusampadaṃ disvā
tattha[^046-1] viharamāno: sattavassiko dāni dārako, kālo naṃ
pabbājetun ti cintetvā rājadhītāya ārocāpetvā Nigrodha-
kumāraṃ pabbājesi. kumāro khuragge yeva arahattaṃ
pāpuṇi. so ekadivasaṃ pāto 'va sarīraṃ jaggetvā[^046-2] ācariyu-
pajjhāyavattaṃ katvā pattacīvaram ādāya mātu[^046-3] upāsikāya
gehadvāraṃ gacchāmīti nikkhami. mātu nivesanaṭṭhānañ[^046-4]
c' assa dakkhiṇadvārena naṅgaraṃ[^046-5] pavisitvā nagaramaj-
jhena gantvā pācīnadvārena nikkhamitvā gantabbaṃ hoti.
tena ca samayena Asoko dhammarājā pācīnadisābhi-
mukho sīhapañjare[^046-6] caṅkamati. taṃkhaṇaṃ yeva[^046-7] Nigro-
dho rājaṅgaṇaṃ sampāpuṇi santdriyo santamānaso yuga-
mattaṃ pekkhamāno. tena vuttaṃ: ekadivasaṃ[^046-8] sīhapañ-
jare ṭhito addasa Nigrodhaṃ[^046-9]sāmaṇeraṃ rājaṅgaṇena gac-
chantaṃ dantaṃ guttaṃ santindriyaṃ[^046-10] iriyāpathasampannan
ti.[^046-11] disvā pan' assa[^046-12] etad ahosi: ayaṃ jano sabbo pi vikkhit-
tacitto bhantamigapaṭibhāgo,[^046-13] ayaṃ pana dārako avikkhit-
tacitto ativiya c' assa[^046-14] ālokitavilokitaṃ sammiñjanapasāra-
ṇañ[^046-15] ca sobhati, addhā etassa abbhantare lokuttaradhammo
bhavissatīti rañño saha dassanen' eva sāmaṇere cittaṃ pasīdi,
pemaṃ saṇṭhahi.[^046-16]
kasmā. pubbe pi[^046-17] kira puññakaraṇakāle esa rañño jeṭṭha-
bhātā[^046-18] vāṇijako[^046-19] ahosi. vuttam pi c' etaṃ[^046-20]:

##### *[PTS Page 047]*

pubbeva sannivāsena paccuppannahitena vā
     evaṃ taṃ jāyate pemaṃ uppalaṃ[^047-1] va yathodake ti.
     atha rājā sañjātapemo sabahumāno[^047-2], etaṃ sāmaṇeraṃ
pakkosathā 'ti amacce pesesi. te aticirāyantīti puna dve
tayo pesesi turitaṃ āgacchatū 'ti.
sāmaṇero attano pakatiyā yeva[^047-3] agamāsi. rājā, patirūpam
āsanaṃ ñatvā nisīdathā 'ti āha. so ito c' ito ca viloketvā
n' atthi dāni aññe bhikkhū ti samussitasetacchattaṃ rājapal-
laṅkaṃ upasaṃkamitvā pattagahaṇatthāya[^047-4] rañño ākāraṃ
dassesi. rājā taṃ pallaṅkasamīpaṃ upagacchantaṃ eva[^047-5]
disvā cintesi: ajj' eva dāni ayaṃ sāmaṇero imassa gehassa
sāmiko bhavissatīti.
sāmaṇero rañño hatthe pattaṃ datvā pallaṇkaṃ abhirū-
hitvā nisīdi. rājā attano atthāya sampāditaṃ sabbaṃ yā-
gukhajjakabhattavikatiṃ upanāmesi.
sāmaṇero khādaniyabhojaniyaṃ[^047-6] attano yāpaniyamattam[^047-7]
eva sampaṭicchi. bhattakiccāvasāne rājā āha: satthārā
tumhākaṃ dinnaovādaṃ[^047-8] jānāthā 'ti.
jānāmi[^047-9] mahārāja ekadesenā 'ti. tāta mayham pi naṃ
kathehīti. sādhu mahārājā 'ti rañño anurūpaṃ Dhammapade
appamādavaggaṃ anumodanatthāya abhāsi. rājā pana
‘appamādo amatapadaṃ[^047-10] pamādo maccuno padan' ti[^047-11]
sutvā 'va aññātaṃ tāta pariyosāpehīti āha. anumodanāva-
sāne,[^047-12] aṭṭha te tāta dhuvabhattāni[^047-13] dammīti āha. sāmaṇero
āha: etāni ahaṃ upajjhāyassa dammi mahārājā 'ti. ko
ayaṃ tāta upajjhāyo nāmā 'ti. vajjāvajjaṃ disvā codetā
sāretā[^047-14] mahārājā 'ti. aññāni pi te tāta[^047-15] aṭṭha dammīti.

##### *[PTS Page 048]*

etāni ācariyassa dammi mahārājā 'ti. ko ayaṃ tāta ācariyo
nāmā 'ti. imasmiṃ sāsane sikkhitabbakadhammesu patiṭ-
ṭhāpetā mahārājā 'ti. sādhu tāta aññāni pi te aṭṭha dammīti.
etāni bhikkhusaṃghassa dammi mahārājā 'ti. ko ayaṃ
tāta bhikkhusaṃgho nāmā 'ti. yaṃ nissāya mahārāja
amhākaṃ ācariyupajjhāyānañ ca mamañ ca pabbajjā ca
upasampadā cā 'ti. rājā bhiyyosomattāya[^048-1] tuṭṭhacitto āha:
aññāni pi te tāta aṭṭha dammīti. sāmaṇero sādhū 'ti sampa-
ṭicchitvā punadivase dvattiṃsa bhikkhū gahetvā rājante-
puraṃ pavisitvā bhattakiccaṃ akāsi. rājā[^048-2] aññe pi dvattiṃsa
bhikkhū tumhehi saddhiṃ sve[^048-3] bhikkhaṃ gaṇhantū 'ti eten'
eva upāyena[^048-4] divase divase vaḍḍhāpento saṭṭhisahassānaṃ
brāhmaṇaparibbājakādīnaṃ bhattaṃ upacchinditvānto
nivesane saṭṭhisahassānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ niccabhattaṃ paṭ-
ṭhapesi Nigrodhatthere gaten' eva pasādena.
Nigrodhatthero pi rājānaṃ saparisaṃ tīsu saraṇesu pañ-
casu ca sīlesu patiṭṭhāpetvā buddhasāsane pothujjanikena
pasādena acalappasādaṃ katvā patiṭṭhāpesi.
puna rājā Asokārāmaṃ nāma mahāvihāraṃ kārāpetva[^048-5]
saṭṭhisahassānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ niccabhattaṃ paṭṭhapesi.
sakala-Jambudīpe caturāsītiyā naṅgarasahassesu[^048-6] caturāsīti
vihārasahassāni kārāpesi caturāsītisahassacetiyapatimaṇḍi-
tāni dhammen' eva no adhammena.
ekadivasaṃ kira rājā Asokārāme mahādānaṃ datvā
saṭṭhisahassasaṃkhassa[^048-7] bhikkhusaṃghassa majjhe nisajja
saṃghaṃ catūhi[^048-8] paccayehi pavāretvā imaṃ[^048-9] pañhaṃ puc-
chi: bhante bhagavatā desitadhammo[^048-10] nāma kittako hotīti.
aṅgato mahārāja navaṅgāni,[^048-11] khandhato[^048-12] caturāsīti-
dhammakkhandhasahassānīti. rājā dhamme pasīditvā[^048-13] eka-
mekaṃ[^048-14] dhammakkhandhaṃ ekekavihārena pūjessāmīti,[^048-15]

ekadivasam eva channavutikoṭidhanaṃ vissajjetvā[^049-1] amacce
āṇāpesi[^049-2]: etha bhaṇe[^049-3] ekamekasmiṃ nagare ekamekaṃ vi-
hāraṃ kārāpentā[^049-4] caturāsītiyā naṅgarasahassesu[^049-5] caturāsīti-
vihārasahassāni kārāpethā 'ti, sayañ ca Asokārāme Asoka-
mahāvihāratthāya kammaṃ paṭṭhapesi.
     saṃgho Indaguttattheraṃ nāma mahiddhikaṃ[^049-6] mahānu-
bhāvaṃ khīṇāsavaṃ navakammādhiṭṭhāyakaṃ adāsi. thero
yaṃ yaṃ kammaṃ[^049-7] na niṭṭhāti taṃ taṃ attano ānubhāvena
niṭṭhāpesi. evam pi tīhi saṃvaccharehi vihārakammaṃ niṭ-
ṭhāpesi.
ekadivasam eva sabbanagarehi paṇṇāni āgamimsu; amaccā
rañño ārocesum: niṭṭhitāni deva caturāsītivihārasahassānīti.
     ājā naṅgare[^049-8] bheriñ carāpesi: ito sattannaṃ divasānaṃ
accayena vihāramaho bhavissati, sabbe aṭṭha sīlaṅgāni sa-
mādiyitvā antonaṅgare ca bahinaṅgare ca vihāramahaṃ
paṭiyādentū 'ti.
     tato sattannaṃ divasānaṃ accayena sabbālaṅkāravibhūsi-
tāya anekasatasahassasaṃkhāya[^049-9] caturaṅginīsenāya[^049-10] pari-
vuto devaloke Amaravatiyā rājadhāniyā[^049-11] sirito adhikatara-
sassirikaṃ[^049-12] viya naṅgaraṃ kātukāmena ussāhajātena ma-
hājanena[^049-13] alaṃkatapaṭiyattaṃ naṅgaraṃ[^049-14] anuvicaranto vi-
hāraṃ gantvā bhikkhusaṃghassa majjhe aṭṭhāsi.
tasmiñ ca khaṇe sannipatitā asītibhikkhukoṭiyo ahesuṃ
bhikkhunīnañ ca channavutisatasahassāni. tattha khīṇāsa-
vabhikkhū yeva satasahassasaṃkhā[^049-15] ahesuṃ. tesaṃ etad
ahosi: sace rājā attano adhikāraṃ anavasesaṃ passeyya,
ativiya buddhasāsane pasīdeyyā 'ti.

tato lokavivaraṇaṃ nāma pāṭihāriyaṃ[^050-1] akaṃsu. rājā
Asokārāme ṭhito 'va catuddisā anuvilokento samantato sa-
muddapariyantaṃ Jambudīpaṃ passati caturāsītiñ ca[^050-2] vi-
hārasahassāni passati[^050-3] uḷārāya vihāramahapūjāya[^050-4] viroca-
mānāni.
so taṃ vibhūtiṃ passamāno uḷārena pītipāmojjena saman-
nāgato, atthi pana aññassa pi kassaci evarūpaṃ pītipāmojjaṃ
uppāpubban ti cintento bhikkhusaṃghaṃ pucchi: bhante
amhākaṃ lokanāthassa[^050-5] dasabalassa sāsane ko mahāparic-
cāgaṃ pariccaji, kassa pariccāgo mahanto ti vadantīti. bhik-
khusaṃgho Moggaliputtatissattherassa bhāraṃ akāsi. thero
āha: mahārāja dasabalassa sāsane paccayadāyako nāma tayā
sadiso dharamāne pi tathāgate pi[^050-6] na koci ahosi, tav' eva
pariccāgo mahā ti. rājā therassa vacanaṃ sutvā uḷārena
pītipāmojjena nirantaraṃ phuṭasarīro hutvā cintesi: n' atthi
kira mayā sadiso paccayadāyako, mayhaṃ kira pariccāgo
mahā, ahaṃ kira deyyadhammena sāsanaṃ paggaṇhāmīti.[^050-7]
kiṃ panāhaṃ evaṃ sati sāsanassa dāyādo homi na homīti.
tato bhikkhusaṃghaṃ pucchi: bhavāmi nu kho ahaṃ
bhante sāsanassa dāyādo ti. tato Moggaliputtatissatthero
rañño idaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā rājaputtassa Mahindassa[^050-8] upa-
nissayasampattiṃ sampassamāno, sace ayaṃ kumāro pab-
bajissati sāsanassa ativiya vuddhi[^050-9] bhavissatīti cintetvā rā-
jānaṃ etad avoca: na kho mahārāja sāsanassa ettāvatā[^050-10]
dāyādo hoti, api ca kho paccayadāyako ti vā upaṭṭhāyako[^050-11]
ti vā saṃkhaṃ[^050-12] gacchati. yo pi hi[^050-13] mahārāja paṭhavito[^050-14]
yāva brahmalokaparimāṇam paccayarāsiṃ dadeyya so pi
sāsane dāyādo ti saṃkhaṃ[^050-15] na gacchatīti. atha, kathañ
carahi bhante sāsanassa dāyādo hotīti. yo hi[^050-16] koci mahārāja

aḍḍho vā daliddo[^051-1] vā attano orasaṃ puttaṃ pabbājeti,
ayaṃ vuccati mahārāja dāyādo sāsanassā 'ti. evaṃ vutte
Asoko rājā[^051-2]: ahaṃ kira evarūpaṃ pariccāgaṃ katvāpi n' eva
sāsanassa dāyādabhāvaṃ patto[^051-3] ti sāsane dāyādabhāvaṃ
patthayamāno ito c' ito ca viloketvā[^051-4] addasa Mahindaku-
māraṃ[^051-5] avidūre ṭhitaṃ. disvā pan' assa[^051-6] etad ahosi: kiñcāpi
ahaṃ imaṃ kumāraṃ Tissakumārassa pabbajitakālato
pabhuti oparajje[^051-7] patiṭṭhāpetukāmo,[^051-8] atha kho oparajjato
pi pabbajjā va uttamā ti.
tato kumāraṃ āha: sakkhissasi[^051-9] tvaṃ tāta pabbajitun ti.
kumāro pana[^051-10] pakatiyāpi Tissakumārassa pabbajitakālato
pabhuti pabbajitukāmo va rañño vacanaṃ sutvā ativiya
pāmujjajāto[^051-11] hutvā āha: pabbajāmi deva maṃ pabbājetvā
tumhe sāsane dāyādo[^051-12] hothā 'ti. tena ca samayena rāja-
dhītā Saṃghamittā pi tasmiṃ yeva ṭhāne[^051-13] ṭhitā[^051-14] hoti. tassā
ca sāmiko Aggibrahmā nāma kumāro uparājena[^051-15] Tissakumā-
rena saddhiṃ pabbajito hoti. rājā taṃ disvā āha: tvam pi
amma pabbajituṃ sakkhissasīti.[^051-16] sādhu tāta sakkomīti.
rājā puttānaṃ manaṃ labhitvā pahaṭṭhacitto bhikkhusaṃ-
ghaṃ etad avoca: bhante ime dārake pabbājetvā maṃ sāsane
dāyādaṃ karothā 'ti.
 aṃgho rañño vacanaṃ sampaṭicchitvā kumāraṃ Mogga-
liputtatissattherena upajjhāyena Mahādevattherena[^051-17] ca āca-
riyena pabbājesi,[^051-18] Majjhantikattherena ācariyena upasam-
pādesi. tadā kira kumāro paripuṇṇavīsativasso va hoti.
So tasmiṃ yeva upasampadāsīmāmaṇḍale[^051-19] saha paṭisam-
bhidāhi arahattaṃ pāpuṇi. Saṃghamittāya pi rājadhītāya
ācariyā Āpālittherī[^051-20] nāma upajjhāyā pana Dhammapālit-
therī[^051-21] nāma ahosi.

tadā Saṃghamittā aṭṭhārasa vassāni[^052-1] hoti. taṃ pabbaji-
tamattaṃ tasmiṃ yeva sīmāmaṇḍale[^052-2] sikkhāya patiṭṭhā-
pesuṃ. ubhinnaṃ[^052-3] pabbajitakāle rājā chavassābhiseko hoti.
atha Mihindatthero upasampannakālato pabhuti attano
upajjhāyass' eva santike dhammañ ca vinayañ ca pariyā-
puṇanto dve pi saṃgītiyo ārūḷhaṃ tipiṭakasaṃgahītaṃ[^052-4] saṭ-
ṭhakathaṃ[^052-5] sabbattheravādaṃ[^052-6] tiṇṇaṃ vassānaṃ abbhantare
uggahetvā attano upajjhāyassa antevāsikānaṃ sahassamat-
tānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pāmokkho ahosi.
tadā Asoko dhammarājā navavassābhiseko hoti. rañño
pana aṭṭhavassābhisekakāle yeva Kontiputtatissatthero[^052-7] vyā-
dhipaṭikammatthaṃ bhikkhācāravattena āhiṇḍanto[^052-8] pasa-
tamattaṃ sappiṃ alabhitvā vyādhibalena parikkhīṇāyusaṃ-
khāro bhikkhusaṃghaṃ appamādena ovaditvā[^052-9] ākāse pal-
laṅkena nisīditvā tejodhātuṃ samāpajjitvā parinibbāyi.
rājā taṃ pavattiṃ sutvā therassa sakkāraṃ katvā, mayi
nāma rajjaṃ kārente evaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paccayā dullabhā
ti naṅgarassa catusu[^052-10] dvāresu pokkharaṇiyo kārāpetvā[^052-11]
bhesajjassa pūrāpetvā dāpesi.
tena kira samayena Pāṭaliputtassa[^052-12] catusu[^052-10] dvāresu cattāri
satasahassāni, sabhāyaṃ[^052-13] satasahassan ti divase divase
pañcasatasahassāni rañño uppajjanti, tato rājā Nigrodhat-
therassa devasikaṃ satasahassaṃ vissajjesi, buddhassa tiye
gandhamālādīhi pūjanatthāya satasahassaṃ, dhammassa
satasahassaṃ, taṃ dhammadharānaṃ bahussutānaṃ catu-
paccayatthāya upanīyati,[^052-14] saṃghassa satasahassaṃ, catusu
dvāresu bhesajjatthāya[^052-15] satasahassaṃ. evaṃ sāsane uḷāro

##### *[PTS Page 053]*

lābhasakkāro nibbatti. titthiyā parihīnalābhasakkārā an-
tamaso ghāsacchādanam pi alabhantā lābhasakkāraṃ pat-
thayamānā sāsane pabbajitvā sakāni sakāni diṭṭhigatāni
ayaṃ dhammo ayaṃ vinayo ti dīpenti. pabbajjaṃ alabha-
mānāpi[^053-1] sayam eva muṇḍetvā kāsāyāni vatthāni[^053-2] acchādetvā
vihāresu vicarantā uposatham pi pavāraṇam pi saṃghakam-
mam pi gaṇakammam pi pavisanti. bhikkhū tehi saddhiṃ
uposatham na karonti.
tadā Moggaliputtatissatthero, uppannaṃ dāni idaṃ adhi-
karaṇaṃ, taṃ na cirass'[^053-3] eva kakkhalaṃ[^053-4] bhavissati, na kho
pan' etaṃ sakkā imesaṃ majjhe vasantena vūpasametun[^053-5] ti
Mahindattherassa gaṇaṃ niyyādetvā[^053-6] attano[^053-7] phāsukavihā-
rena[^053-8] viharitukāmo Ahogaṅgāpabbataṃ[^053-9] agamāsi.
te pi kho titthiyā bhikkhusaṃghena dhammena vinayena
satthusāsanena[^053-10] niggayhamānāpi dhammavinayānulomāya
paṭipattiyā asandhahantā[^053-11] anekarūpaṃ sāsanassa abbudañ
ca malañ ca kaṇṭakañ ca samuṭṭhāpesuṃ. keci aggiṃ pari-
caranti, keci pañcātape tappanti,[^053-12] keci ādiccaṃ anuparivat-
tanti, keci dhammañ ca vinayañ ca vobhindissāmā 'ti paggaṇ-
hiṃsu.
tadā bhikkhusaṃgho na tehi saddhiṃ uposathaṃ vā pa-
vāraṇaṃ vā ākāsi. Asokārame satta vassāni uposatho upac-
chijji. rañño pi etam atthaṃ ārocesuṃ. rājā ekaṃ amaccaṃ
āṇāpesi, vihāraṃ gantvā adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasametvā[^053-13] upo-

rājā anekappakāram yācitvā[^055-7] tam nivattetum asakkonto

yuvarājā kira pabbajissatīti sutvā bahubhikkhū[^055-10] patta-

kumāro rañño catuvassābhisekakāle pabbajito. ath'
añño pi rañño bhāgineyyo Saṃghamittāya sāmiko Aggi-

thero sve Pāṭaliputtaṃ sampāpuṇissatīti rattibhāge rājā
supinaṃ addasa. evarūpo supino ahosi: sabbaseto hatthi-
nāgo āgantvā rājānaṃ sīsato paṭṭhāya parāmasitvā dakkhi-
ṇahatthe aggahesi.[^058-1] punadivase rājā supinajjhāyake pucchi:
mayā evarūpo supino diṭṭho, kim me bhavissatīti. eko taṃ
mahārāja samaṇanāgo dakkhiṇahatthe gaṇhissatīti. atha
rājā tāvad eva[^058-2], thero āgato ti sutvā Gaṅgātīraṃ gantvā
nadiṃ otaritvā abbhuggacchanto jānumatte[^058-3] udake theraṃ
sampāpuṇitvā therassa nāvāto otarantassa hatthaṃ adāsi.
thero rājānaṃ dakkhiṇahatthe aggahesi. taṃ disvā asiggā-
hā[^058-4] therassa sīsaṃ pātessāmā 'ti kosito[^058-5] asiṃ abbāhiṃsu.[^058-6]
kasmā, etaṃ kira cārittaṃ rājakulesu yo[^058-7] rājānaṃ hatthe
gaṇhāti[^058-8] tassa asinā sīsaṃ pātetabban ti. rājā chāyaṃ
yeva disvā āha: pubbe pi ahaṃ bhikkhūsu viruddhakāraṇā[^058-9]
assāsaṃ[^058-10] na vindāmi, mā kho there virajjhitthā 'ti.[^058-11] thero
pana kasmā rājānaṃ hatthe aggahesīti. yasmā raññā pañ-
haṃ pucchanatthāya pakkosāpito, tasmā antevāsiko me
ayan ti aggahesi. rājā theraṃ attano uyyānaṃ netvā bā-
hirato tikkhattuṃ parivārāpetvā ārakkhaṃ ṭhapetvā sayam
eva therassa pāde dhovitvā telena[^058-12] makkhetvā therassa[^058-13]
santike nisīditvā, paṭibalo nu kho thero mama kaṅkhaṃ
chiṅditvā uppannaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasametvā sāsanaṃ
paggaṇhitun ti vīmaṃsanatthāya, ahaṃ bhante ekaṃ pāṭi-
hāriyaṃ daṭṭhukāmo 'ti āha. kataraṃ[^058-14] pāṭihāriyaṃ daṭ-
ṭhukāmo 'si maharājā 'ti. paṭhavikampanaṃ[^058-15] bhante 'ti.
sakalapaṭhavikampanaṃ[^058-16] daṭṭhukāmo 'si mahārāja[^058-17] padesa-
paṭhavikampanan[^058-16] ti. kataraṃ pan' ettha bhante dukkaran

udakaṃ kampetuṃ dukkaraṃ udāhu[^058-18] upaḍḍhan ti. upaḍ-
ḍhaṃ bhante ti. evam eva kho[^058-19] mahārāja padesapaṭhavi-
kampanaṃ[^058-15] dukkaran ti. tena hi bhante padesapaṭhavi[^058-16]

mattā pabbajiṃsu. evaṃ te tattha sāsaṇaṃ paṭiṭṭhāpesuṃ.

     yakkhasenaṃ pakāsento Dhammacakkappavattanan[^068-6] ti.
Sonakatthero[^068-7] pi saddhiṃ Uttarattherena Suvaṇṇabhūmiṃ
agamāsi. tena ca samayena tattha ekā rakkhasī samuddato
nikkhamitvā rājakule jāte jāte dārake khādati. taṃ divasam
eva rājakule eko dārako jāto hoti. manussā theraṃ disvā,
rakkhasānaṃ sahāyako eso ti maññamānā āvudhāni[^068-8] gahetvā
theraṃ pariharitukāmā[^068-9] gacchanti.[^068-10] thero kiṃ tumhe
āvudhahatthā[^068-11] āgacchathā'ti āha. te āhaṃsu: rājakule jāte
jāte dārake rakkhasā khādanti, tesaṃ tumhe sahāyakā ti.
thero, na mayaṃ rakkhasānaṃ[^068-12] sahāyakā, samaṇā nāma ma-
yaṃ viratā pāṇātipātā --pe-- viratā majjapānā ekabhattikā
sīlavanto kalyāṇadhammā ti āha. tasmiṃ yeva ca khaṇe sā
rakkhasī saparivārā samuddato nikkhami, rājakule dārako
jāto taṃ khādissāmīti. manussā taṃ disvā, esā bhante
rakkhasī[^068-13] āgacchatīti[^068-14]bhītā[^068-15] viraviṃsu. thero rakkhasehi
diguṇe[^068-16] attabhāve nimminitvā tehi attabhāvehi taṃ rakkha-

siṃ saparisaṃ majjhe katvā ubhosu passesu parikkhipi.[^069-1]

ayo ahosi, kuladārakānaṃ aḍḍhuḍḍhāni sahassāni pabbajiṃsu,

Mahindatthero pana, Tambapaṇṇidīpaṃ gantvā sāsanaṃ
patiṭṭhāpehīti upajjhāyena ca bhikkhusaṃghena ca ajjhiṭṭho
cintesi: kālo nu kho me Tambapaṇṇidīpaṃ gantuṃ[^069-11] no ti.
ath' assa vīmaṃsato, na tāva kālo ti ahosi. kiṃ pan' assa
disvā etad ahosi. Muṭasīvarañño[^069-12] mahallakabhāvaṃ. tato
cintesi: ayaṃ rājā mahallako, na sakkā imaṃ gaṇhitvā sā-
sanaṃ paggahetuṃ, idāni pan' assa putto Devānampiyatisso
rajjaṃ kāressati taṃ gaṇhitvā sakkā bhavissati sāsanaṃ
paggahetuṃ. handa yāva so samayo āgacchati tāva ñātake
olokema, puna dāni mayaṃ imaṃ janapadaṃ āgaccheyyāma
vā na vā ti. so evaṃ cintetvā upajjhāyañ ca bhikkhusaṃghañ
ca vanditvā Asokārāmato nikkhamma tehi Iddhiyādīhi[^069-13] ca-
tūhi[^069-14] therehi Saṃghamittāya puttena Sumanasāmaṇerena

Bhaṇḍukena ca upāsakena saddhiṃ Rājagahanagaraparivat-
takena Dakkhiṇagirijanapade cārikaṃ[^070-1] caramāno ñātake
olokento cha māse atikkāmesi.[^070-2] athānupubbena mātu
nivesanaṭṭhānaṃ[^070-3] Vedisanagaraṃ[^070-4] nāma[^070-5] sampatto. Asoko
kira kumārakāle janapadaṃ labhitvā Ujjeniṃ gacchanto
Vedisanagaraṃ[^070-6] patvā Devaseṭṭhissa[^070-7] dhītaraṃ aggahesi. sā
taṃ divasam eva gabbhaṃ gaṇhitvā Ujjeniyaṃ Mahindaku-
māraṃ vijāyi. kumārassa cuddasavassakāle rājā abhisekaṃ
pāpuṇi. sā tassa mātā tena samayena ñātighare vasati.
tena vuttaṃ: anupubbena[^070-8] mātu nivesanaṭṭhānaṃ[^070-9] Vedisa-
nagaraṃ nāma sampatto ti. sampattañ ca pana theraṃ
disvā theramātā Devī pāde[^070-10] sirasā[^070-11] vanditvā bhikkhaṃ datvā
theraṃ attano[^070-12] kataṃ Vedisagirimahāvihāraṃ nāma āropesi.
thero tasmiṃ vihāre nisinno cintesi: amhākaṃ idha kattabba-
kiccaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ, samayo nu kho idāni Laṅkādīpaṃ gantun
ti. tato cintesi: anubhavatu tāva me pitarā[^070-13] pesitaṃ abhi-
sekaṃ Devānampiyatisso ratanattayaguṇañ ca suṇātu cha-
ṇatthañ ca[^070-14] nagarato nikkhamitvā Missakapabbataṃ abhi-
rūhatu,[^070-15] tadā taṃ tattha dakkhissāmā 'ti. athāparam eka-
māsaṃ tatth' eva saṃkappesi.[^070-16] māsātikkamena ca jeṭṭhamū-
lamāsapuṇṇamāyaṃ uposathadivase sannipatitā sabbe pi,
kālo nu kho amhākaṃ Tambapaṇṇidīpaṃ[^070-17] gamanāya udāhu
no ti[^070-18] mantayiṃsu. tenāhu porāṇā:
     Mahindo nāma nāmena saṃghatthero tadā ahū,[^070-19]
     Iddhiyo[^070-20] Vuttiyo[^070-21] thero Bhaddasālo ca Sambalo;

evaṃ Iddhiyādīhi[^072-1] saddhiṃ āgantvā patiṭṭhahanto ca
āyasmā Mahindatthero sammāsambuddhassa parinibbānato
dvinnaṃ[^072-2] vassasatānaṃ upari chattiṃsatime vasse imasmiṃ
dīpe patiṭṭhahīti veditabbaṃ.[^072-3]
Ajātasattussa hi aṭṭhame vasse sammāsambuddho pari-
nibbāyi. tasmiṃ yeva vasse[^072-4] Sīhakumārassa putto Tamba-
paṇṇidīpassa ādirājā Vijayakumāro imaṃ dīpam āgantvā
manussavāsaṃ[^072-5] akāsi. Jambudīpe Udayabhadrassa[^072-6] cud-
dasame vasse idha[^072-7] Vijayo kālam akāsi. Udayabhadrassa
pañcadasame vasse Paṇḍuvāsadevo[^072-8] nāma imasmiṃ dīpe
rajjaṃ pāpuṇi. tattha Nāgadassakarañño[^072-9] vīsatime vasse
idha Paṇḍuvāsadevo[^072-10] tiṃsavassāni rajjaṃ kāretvā[^072-11] kālam
akāsi. tasmiṃ[^072-12] vasse Abhayo nāma rājakumāro[^072-13] imasmiṃ
dīpe rajjaṃ pāpuṇi. tattha Susunāgarañño sattarasame vasse
idha Abhayarañño vīsaṃ[^072-14] vassāni paripūriṃsu. atha Abha-
yassa vīsatime vasse Pakuṇḍakābhayo nāma dāmariko rajjam
aggahesi. tattha Kālāsokassa[^072-15] soḷasame vasse idha Pa-
kuṇḍakābhayassa[^072-16] sattarasa vassāni paripūriṃsu. tāni heṭ-
ṭhā ekena vassena saha aṭṭhārasa honti. tattha Candagut-
tassa cuddasame vasse idha Pakuṇḍakābhayo kālam akāsi,
Muṭasīvarājā[^072-17] rajjaṃ pāpuṇi. tattha Asokadhammarājassa
sattarasame vasse idha[^072-18] Muṭasīvarājā[^072-19] kālam akāsi, Devā-
naṃpiyatisso rajjaṃ pāpuṇi. [^072-20]parinibbute ca pana sam-
buddhe[^072-20] Ajātasattu catuvīsativassāni rajjaṃ kāresi, Udaya-

dhassa parinibbānato dvinnaṃ vassasatānaṃ upari chattiṃ-

tasmiñ ca divase Tambapaṇṇidīpe jeṭṭhamūlanakkhattaṃ
nāma nakkhattaṃ[^073-15] hoti. rājā nakkhattaṃ ghosāpetvā
chaṇaṃ karothā 'ti amacce[^073-16] āṇāpetvā[^073-17] cattālīsapurisasahassa-

pāyāsi migavaṃ kīḷitukāmo.[^073-18] atha tasmiṃ pabbate adhi-
vatthā ekā devatā rañño there dassessāmīti rohitamigarūpaṃ
gahetvā avidūre tiṇapaṇṇāni khādamānā[^073-19] viya carati. rājā
disvā,[^073-20] ayuttaṃ dāni pamattaṃ vijjhitun ti jiyaṃ[^073-21] poṭhesi.
migo Ambatthalamaggaṃ gahetvā palāyituṃ ārabhi. rājā

satu mā itare ti adhiṭṭhahitvā, Tissa Tissa ito ehīti āha. rājā

hetvā ālapituṃ samattho nāma n' atthi. ayaṃ pana chinna-
bhinnapaṭadharo bhaṇḍukāsāvavasano maṃ nāmena ālapati.
ko nu kho ayaṃ bhavissati manusso vā amanusso vā ti. thero
āha:
     samaṇā mayaṃ mahārāja dhammarājassa sāvakā
     tam[^074-1] eva anukampāya Jambudīpā idhāgatā ti.
tena ca samayena Devānampiyatisso[^074-2] mahārājā ca Asoka-
dhammarājā ca adiṭṭhasahāyakā honti. Devānampiyatissa-
mahārājassa ca puññānubhāvena Chātakapabbatapāde[^074-3]
ekamhi veṇugumbe[^074-4] tisso veṇuyaṭṭhiyo[^074-4] rathayaṭṭhippamāṇā
uppajjiṃsu, ekā latāyaṭṭhi nāma, ekā pupphayaṭṭhi nāma,
ekā sakuṇayaṭṭhi nāma. tāsu latāyaṭṭhiyaṃ[^074-5] rajatavaṇṇā
hoti, taṃ alaṃkaritvā uppannalatā kañcanavaṇṇā khāyati.
pupphayaṭṭhiyaṃ[^074-6] pana nīlapītalohitāvadātakāḷavaṇṇāni[^074-7]
pupphāni suvibhattavaṇṭapattakiñjakkhāni[^074-8] hutvā khāyanti,
sakuṇayaṭṭhiyaṃ haṃsakukkuṭajīvajīvakādayo[^074-9] sakuṇā
nānappakārāni[^074-10] catuppadāni[^074-11] sajīvāni[^074-12] viya khāyanti.
vuttam pi c' etaṃ Dīpavaṃse[^074-13]:
     Chātapabbatapādamhi veṇuyaṭṭhi[^074-14] tayo ahu[^074-15]:
     setā rajatayaṭṭhi ca[^074-16] latā kañcanasannibhā,
     nīlādi yādisaṃ[^074-17] pupphaṃ[^074-18] pupphayaṭṭhiṃhi[^074-19] tādisaṃ,
     sakuṇā sakuṇayaṭṭhimhi sarūpen' eva saṇṭhitā ti.

aruṇavaṇṇamattikañ[^076-1] ca añjanaṃ[^076-2] nāgamāhaṭaṃ.

     puññakammābhinibbattaṃ pāhesi Asokasavhayo[^076-5] ti.
na kevalañ ca etaṃ[^076-6] āmisapaṇṇākāraṃ imaṃ kira dhamma-
paṇṇākāram pi pesesi:
     ahaṃ buddhañ ca dhammañ ca saṃghañ ca saraṇaṃ
gato
     upāsakattaṃ vedesiṃ[^076-7] Sakyaputtassa sāsane.
     imesu tīsu vatthūsu uttame[^076-8] jinasāsane[^076-9]
     tvam pi cittaṃ pasādehi saddhāsaraṇam upehisīti.[^076-10]
sv' āyam rājā taṃ divasaṃ Asokarañño[^076-11] pesitena abhisekena

akaṃsu. so acirasutaṃ taṃ sāsanapavattiṃ[^076-14] anussaramāno

āha. mayā saddhiṃ yeva mahārājā 'ti. idāni pana Jambu-
dīpe aññe pi evarūpā samaṇā santīti.[^077-1] santi mahārāja, eta-
rahi Jambudīpo kāsāvapajjoto isivātaparivāto,[^077-2] tasmiṃ.
     tevijjā iddhippattā ca cetopariyāyakovidā[^077-3]
     khīṇāsavā arahantā[^077-4] bahū[^077-5] buddhassa sāvakā ti.
bhante kena āgata 'tthā 'ti. n' eva mahārāja udakena[^077-6]
na thalenā 'ti. rājā ākāsena āgatā 'ti aññāsi. thero atthi
nu kho rañño[^077-7] paññāveyyattikan[^077-8] ti vīmaṃsanatthāya[^077-9]
āsannāmbarukkhaṃ[^077-10] ārabbha pañhaṃ pucchi: kiṃnāmo
ayaṃ mahārāja rukkho ti. ambarukkho[^077-11] nāma bhante ti.
imaṃ[^077-12] pana mahārāja ambaṃ muñcitvā añño ambo[^077-13] atthi
n' atthīti. atthi bhante aññe pi bahū ambarukkhā ti. imañ
ca ambaṃ te ca ambe muñcitvā atthi nu kho mahārāja aññe
rukkhā ti. atthi bhante, te pana na ambarukkhā ti. aññe
ambe ca anambe ca muñcitvā atthi pana añño rukkho ti.
ayam eva bhante ambarukkho ti. sādhu mahārāja paṇḍito
'si.[^077-14] atthi pana te mahārāja ñātakā ti. atthi me[^077-15] bhante
bahū janā 'ti. te muñcitvā aññe keci aññātakāpi atthi ma-
hārājā 'ti. aññātakā bhante ñātakehi bahutarā ti. tava ñā-
take ca aññātake ca muñcitvā atth'[^077-16] añño koci mahārājā 'ti.
ahaṃ[^077-17] bhante. sādhu mahārāja attā[^077-18] nāma attano n' eva
ñātako na aññātako ti. atha thero, paṇḍito rājā sakkhissati
dhammaṃ aññātun ti Cūlahatthipadopamasuttaṃ[^077-19] kathesi.
kathāpariyosāne rājā tīsu saraṇesu patiṭṭhahi saddhim cat-
tālīsāya[^077-20] pāṇasahassehi. taṃ khaṇañ ñeva[^077-21] rañño bhattaṃ

āhariyittha. rājā suttaṃ[^078-1] suṇanto yeva evaṃ[^078-2] aññāsi: na
imesaṃ imasmiṃ kāle bhojanaṃ kappatīti, āpucchitvā[^078-3] pana
bhuñjituṃ ayuttan ti cintetvā, bhuñjissatha bhante ti pucchi.
na mahārāja amhākaṃ imasmiṃ kāle bhojanaṃ kappatīti.
kasmiṃ kāle bhante kappatīti. aruṇuggamanato[^078-4] paṭṭhāya[^078-5]
yāva majjhantikasamayā[^078-6] mahārājā 'ti. gacchāma bhante
nagaran ti. alaṃ mahārāja idh' eva vasissāmā 'ti. sace
bhante tumhe vasatha ayaṃ dārako āgacchatū 'ti. mahārāja
ayaṃ dārako āgataphalo viññātasāsano pabbajjāpekho idāni
pabbajissatīti. rājā tena hi bhante sve rathaṃ[^078-7] pesessāmi
taṃ abhirūhitvā āgaccheyyathā[^078-8] 'ti vatvā vanditvā pakkāmi.[^078-9]
thero acirapakkantassa[^078-10] rañño Sumanasāmaṇeraṃ āmantesi:
ehi tvaṃ Sumana dhammasavaṇassa kālaṃ ghosehīti. bhante
kittakaṃ ṭhānaṃ sāvento ghosemīti. sakalaṃ Tamba-
paṇṇidīpan ti. sādhu bhante ti sāmaṇero abhiññāpādakaṃ
catutthajjhāṇaṃ samāpajjitvā vuṭṭhāya adhiṭṭhahitvā samā-
hitena cittena sakalaṃ[^078-11] Tambapaṇṇidīpaṃ sāvento tikkhat-
tuṃ dhammasavaṇassa kālaṃ[^078-12] ghosesi. rājā taṃ[^078-13] saddaṃ
sutvā therānaṃ santikaṃ pesesi: kim bhante atthi koci
upaddavo'ti. n' atth' amhākaṃ koci upaddavo ti[^078-14] dhamma-
savaṇassa kālaṃ ghosāpayimha buddhavacanaṃ kathetu-
kāma 'mhā 'ti. tañ ca pana sāmaṇerassa saddaṃ sutvā
bhummā[^078-15] devatā saddam anussāvesuṃ, eten' upāyena yāva

##### *[PTS Page 079]*

nāgā ca supaṇṇā ca saraṇesu patiṭṭhahiṃsu. yādiso[^079-1] Sāri-
puttattherassa imaṃ suttantaṃ kathayato devatāsannipāto
ahosi tādiso Mahindattherassāpi jāto.
atha tassā rattiyā accayena rājā therānaṃ rathaṃ pesesi.
sārathi[^079-2] rathaṃ ekamante ṭhapetvā therānaṃ ārocesi: āhaṭo[^079-3]
bhante ratho, abhirūhatha[^079-4] gacchissāmā 'ti. therā, na mayaṃ
rathaṃ abhirūhāmāccha tvaṃ, pacchā mayaṃ āgacchissā-
mā 'ti vatvā vehāsaṃ abbhuggantvā Anurādhapurassa purat-
thimadisāyaṃ Paṭhamacetiyaṭṭhāne[^079-5] otariṃsu. taṃ hi ce-
tiyaṃ[^079-6] therehi paṭhamaṃ otiṇṇaṭṭhāne katattā yeva Paṭha-
macetiyan[^079-5] ti vuccati. rājāpi sārathiṃ pesetvā antonivesane
maṇḍapaṃ paṭiyādethā 'ti amacce āṇāpesi. tāvad eva sabbe
haṭṭhatuṭṭhā ativiya pāsādikaṃ maṇḍapaṃ paṭiyādesuṃ.
puna rājā cintesi: hiyyo[^079-7] thero sīlakkhandhaṃ kathayamāno
uccāsayanamahāsayanaṃ[^079-8] na[^079-9] kappatīti āha. nisīdissanti
nu kho ayyā āsanesu na nisīdissantīti. tass' evaṃ cintayan-
tass' eva so sārathi[^079-10] nagaradvāraṃ sampatto. tato[^079-11] addasa
there[^079-12] paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā kāyabandhanaṃ bandhitvā
cīvaraṃ pārupante. disvā[^079-13] ativiya pasannacitto hutvā
āgantvā rañño ārocesi: āgatā deva therā ti. rājā rathaṃ
ārūḷhā 'ti pucchi. na ārūḷhā deva api ca mama pacchato
nikkhamitvā paṭhamataraṃ āgantvā pācīnadvāre ṭhitā ti.
rājā ratham pi nārūhiṃsū[^079-14]; 'ti sutvā, na dāni ayyā uccāsaya-
naṃ[^079-15] sādiyissantīti cintetvā, tena hi bhaṇe therānaṃ bhum-
mattharaṇasaṃkhepena[^079-16] āsanāni paññāpethā[^079-17] 'ti vatvā paṭi-
pathaṃ agamāsi. amaccā paṭhaviyaṃ taddhikaṃ[^079-18] paññā-
petvā upari kojavakādīni[^079-19] vicitrattharaṇāni[^079-20] paññāpesuṃ.

##### *[PTS Page 080]*

Tambapaṇṇidīpassa sāmikā bhavissantīti vyākariṃsu. rājāpi
gantvā there vanditvā Mahindattherassa hatthato pattaṃ
gahetvā mahatiyā pūjāya ca sakkārena ca there nagaraṃ
pavesetvā antonivesanaṃ pavesesi. thero āsanapaññattiṃ
disvā amhākaṃ sāsaṇaṃ sakala-Laṅkādīpe paṭhavī viya
patthataṃ[^080-1] niccalañ ca hutvā patiṭṭhahissatīti cintento
nisīdi. rājā there paṇītena[^080-2] khādaniyena bhojaniyena[^080-3]
sahatthā santappetvā sampavāretvā,[^080-4] Anulādevipamukhāni[^080-5]
pañca itthisatāni therānaṃ abhivādanaṃ pūjāsakkārañ ca
karontīti[^080-6] pakkosāpetvā ekamantaṃ nisīdi. thero bhat-
takiccāvasāne[^080-7] rañño saparijanassa dhammaratanavassaṃ
vassento[^080-8] Petavatthuṃ Vimānavatthuṃ Saccasaṃyuttañ ca
kathesi. taṃ therassa dhammadesanaṃ sutvā tāni pañca
pi itthisatāni sotāpattiphalaṃ sacchikariṃsu.[^080-9] ye pi te
manussā purimadivase Missakapabbate there addasaṃsu
te tesu tesu[^080-10] ṭhānesu therānaṃ guṇe kathenti, tesaṃ sutvā
mahājanakāyo rājaṅgaṇe[^080-11] sannipatitvā mahāsaddaṃ akāsi.
rājā kiṃ eso saddo 'ti pucchi. nāgarā deva, there daṭṭhuṃ
na labhāmā 'ti vilapanti.[^080-12] rājā, sace idha pavisissanti okāso
na bhavissatīti cintetvā, gacchatha bhaṇe[^080-13] hatthisālaṃ paṭi-
jaggitvā vāḷukaṃ[^080-14] ākiritvā pañcavaṇṇāni pupphāni vikiritvā
celavitānaṃ[^080-15] bandhitvā maṅgalahatthiṭṭhāne therānaṃ
āsanāni paññāpethā[^080-16] 'ti āha. rājāmaccā[^080-17] tathā akaṃsu.
thero tattha gantvā nisīditvā Devadūtasuttantaṃ kathesi.
kathāpariyosāne pāṇasahassaṃ sotāpattiphale patiṭṭhahi.
tato, hatthisālā atisambādhā 'ti dakkhiṇadvāre Nandana-
vanuyyāne[^080-18] āsanaṃ paññāpesuṃ. thero tattha nisīditvā
Āsivisopamasuttaṃ[^080-19] kathesi. tam pi sutvā pāṇasahassaṃ

sotāpattiphalaṃ paṭilabhi. evaṃ āgatadivasato dutiyadivase
aḍḍhateyyānaṃ sahassānaṃ dhammābhisamayo ahosi.
therassa Nandanavane āgatāgatāhi kulitthīhi kulasuṇhāhi[^081-1]
kulakumārīhi saddhiṃ sammodamānass' eva sāyaṇhasamayo
jāto. thero kālaṃ sallakkhetvā, gacchāma dāni Missakapabba-
tan ti uṭṭhahi.[^081-2] amaccā kattha bhante gacchathā 'ti. amhā-
kaṃ nivesanaṭṭhānan[^081-3] ti. te rañño saṃviditaṃ katvā rājā-
numatena āhaṃsu: akālo bhante idāni tattha gantuṃ, idam
eva Nandanavanaṃ uyyānaṃ[^081-4] ayyānam āvāsaṭṭhānaṃ[^081-5] hotū
'ti. alaṃ gacchāmā[^081-6] 'ti. puna rañño vacanenāhaṃsu: rājā
bhante āha, etaṃ Meghavanaṃ nāma uyyānaṃ mama pitu
santakaṃ nagarato nātidūran nāccāsannaṃ gamanāgamana-
sampannaṃ, ettha therā vāsaṃ kappentū 'ti. vasiṃsu therā
Meghavane uyyāne.[^081-7] rājāpi kho tassā rattiyā accayena
therassa samīpaṃ gantvā sukhasayitabhāvaṃ pucchitvā,
kappati bhante bhikkhusaṃghassa ārāmo ti pucchi. thero
kappati mahārājā 'ti vatvā imaṃ suttaṃ āhari: anujānāmi
bhikkhave ārāman ti.[^081-8] rājā tuṭṭho suvaṇṇabhiṅkāraṃ
gahetvā therassa hatthe udakaṃ pātetvMahāmeghavana-
uyyānaṃ[^081-9] adāsi. saha udakapātena paṭhavī kampi. ayaṃ
Mahāvihāre paṭhamo paṭhavikampo[^081-10] ahosi. rājā bhīto
theraṃ pucchi: kasmā bhante paṭhavī kampatīti. mā bhāyi
mahārāja, imasmiṃ dīpe dasabalassa sāsanaṃ patiṭṭhahissati,
idam ca paṭhamaṃ vihāraṭṭhānaṃ bhavissati, tass' etaṃ
pubbanimittan ti. rājā bhiyyosomattāya[^081-11] pasīdi. thero
punadivase pi rājagahe yeva bhuñjitvā Nandanavane Anama-
taggiyāni kathesi. punadivase Aggikkhandhopamasuttaṃ
kathesi. eten' eva upāyena[^081-12] satta divasāni kathesi, aḍḍhana-
vamānaṃ[^081-13] pāṇasahassānaṃ dhammābhisamayo ahosi. tato

therā dasabhātikasamākulaṃ rājakulaṃ[^083-1] pasādetvā mahā-

Cetiyagirimhi paṭhamavassaṃ[^083-3] upagatā dvāsaṭṭhi arahanto
ahesuṃ.
athāyasmā Mahāmahindo vutthavasso pavāretvā kattika-
puṇṇamāyaṃ uposathadivase rājānaṃ etad avoca: mahārāja
amhehi ciradiṭṭho[^083-4] sammāsambuddho, anāthavassaṃ[^083-5] va-
simha, icchāma[^083-6] Jambudīpaṃ gantun ti. rājā,[^083-7] ahaṃ bhante
tumhe catūhi paccayehi upaṭṭhahāmi ayañ ca mahājano
tumhe nissāya tīsu saraṇesu patiṭṭhito, kasmā tumhe ukkaṇ-
ṭhathā[^083-8] 'ti. ciradiṭṭho[^083-4] no mahārāja sammāsambuddho
abhivādanapaccuṭṭhānāñjalikammasāmīcikammakaraṇaṭ-
ṭhānaṃ[^083-9] n' atthi, tena 'mha[^083-10] ukkaṇṭhitā ti. nanu bhante
tumhe avocuttha[^083-11]: parinibbuto sammāsambuddho ti. kiñ
cāpi mahārāja parinibbuto, atha khv assa sarīradhātuyo
tiṭṭhantīti. aññātam bhante thūpapatiṭṭhānaṃ tumhe ākaṅ-
khathā 'ti, karomi bhante thūpūmibhāgaṃ dāni vici-
nātha. api ca bhante dhātuyo kuto lacchāmā 'ti. Sumanena
saddhiṃ mantehi mahārājā 'ti. sādhu bhante ti rājā Suma-
naṃ[^083-12] upasaṃkamitvā pucchi: kuto dāni bhante dhātuyo lac-
chāmā 'ti. Sumano āha: appossukko tvaṃ mahārāja vīthiyo

parijano[^083-14] uposathaṃ samādiyitvā[^083-15] sabbatālāvacare[^083-16] upaṭ-

ṭhāne dhātuyo lacchasīti. sa[^084-1] rājā sādhū 'ti sampaṭicchi.
therā Cetiyagirim eva agamaṃsu. tatrāyasmā Mahindat-
thero Sumanasāmaṇeram āha: gaccha tvaṃ sāmaṇera Jam-
budīpe tava ayyakaṃ Asokaṃ[^084-2] dhammarājānaṃ upasaṃ-
kamitvā mama vacanena evaṃ vadehi: sahāyo te[^084-3] mahārāja
Devānampiyatisso buddhasāsane pasanno thūpaṃ patiṭṭhā
petukāmo, tumhākaṃ kira hatthe dhātuyo[^084-4] atthi, taṃ me
dethā 'ti. taṃ gahetvā Sakkaṃ devarājānaṃ upasaṃka-
mitvā[^084-5] evaṃ vadehi: tumhākaṃ kira mahārāja hatthe dve
dhātuyo[^084-4] atthi, dakkhiṇadāṭhā dakkhiṇakkhakañ[^084-6] ca, tato
tumhe dakkhiṇadāṭhaṃ pūjetha, dakkiṇakkhakaṃ pana
mayhaṃ dethā 'ti. evañ ca naṃ vadehi: kasmā tvaṃ mahārāja
amhe Tambapaṇṇidīpaṃ pahiṇitvā pamajjitthā 'ti. sādhu
bhante ti kho Sumano therassa vacanaṃ sampaṭicchitvā
tāvad eva pattacīvaram ādāya vehāsaṃ abbhuggantvā
Pāṭaliputtadvāre oruyha rañño santikaṃ gantvā tam[^084-7]
attham ārocesi.
rājā tuṭṭho sāmaṇerassa hatthato pattaṃ gahetvā gandhehi
ubbaṭṭetvā[^084-8] varamuttasadisānaṃ dhātūnaṃ pūretvā adāsi.
so taṃ gahetvā Sakkaṃ devarājānaṃ upasaṃkami. Sakko
devarājā sāmaṇeraṃ disvā 'va:[^084-9] kiṃ bhante Sumana āhiṇḍa-
sīti āha. tvaṃ mahārāja amhe Tambapaṇṇidīpaṃ pesetvā
kasmā pamajjasīti.[^084-10] na ppamajjāmi[^084-11] bhante, vadehi kiṃ
karomīti. tumhākaṃ kira hatthe dve dhātuyo atthi dak-
khiṇadāṭhā ca dakkhiṇakkhakañ ca, tato tumhe dakkhiṇa-
dāṭhaṃ pūjetha dakkhiṇakkhakam pana mayhaṃ dethā 'ti.
sādhu bhante 'ti kho Sakko devānam Indo yojanappamāṇaṃ[^084-12]
maṇithūpaṃ[^084-13] ugghāṭetvā[^084-14] dakkhiṇakkhakadhātuṃ nīharit-
vā Sumanassa adāsi. so taṃ gahetvā Cetiyagirimhi yeva
patiṭṭhāsi. atha kho Mahindapamukhā sabbe pi te mahā-

nāgā Asokadhammarājena dinnadhātuyo Cetiyagirimhi yeva[^085-1]
patiṭṭhāpetvā dakkhiṇakkhakaṃ ādāya vaḍḍhamānakac-
chāyāya Mahānāgavanuyyānam agamaṃsu.[^085-2] rājāpi kho
Sumanena vuttappakāraṃ pūjāsakkāraṃ katvā hatthikkhan-
dhavaragato sayaṃ maṅgalahatthimatthake setacchattaṃ
dhārayamāno Mahānāgavanaṃ sampāpuṇi. ath' assa etad
ahosi: sace ayaṃ sammāsambuddhassa dhātu, chattaṃ
apanetu,[^085-3] maṅgalahatthī[^085-4] jannukehi[^085-5] bhūmiyaṃ patiṭṭha-
hatu, dhātucaṅgoṭakaṃ[^085-6] mayhaṃ matthake patiṭṭhātū 'ti.[^085-7]
saha rañño cittuppādena chattaṃ apanami, hatthī jannukehi[^085-8]
patiṭṭhahi, dhātucaṅgoṭakaṃ[^085-9] rañño matthake patiṭṭhahi.[^085-10]

bhātā Mahindatthero evaṃ pahiṇi: rañño kira bhātu jāyā

ṇidīpaṃ gatā. tassa mayhaṃ te pi apassantassa uppanno
soko tava mukhaṃ passantassa[^092-1] vūpasammati. alaṃ amma
mā[^092-2] agamāsīti. bhāriyaṃ me mahārāja bhātuno[^092-3] vacanaṃ,
Anulāpi khattiyā itthisahassaparivutā pabbajjāpurekkhārā[^092-4]
maṃ patimāneti. gacchām' ahaṃ mahārājā 'ti. tena hi
amma mahābodhiṃ gahetvā gacchāhīti.[^092-5] kuto rañño mahā-
bodhi.[^092-6] rājā kira tato pubbe eva dhātugahaṇatthāya[^092-7] anā-
gate Sumane Laṅkādīpaṃ mahābodhiṃ pesetukāmo, kathan
nu kho asatthaghātārahaṃ mahābodhiṃ pesessāmīti upāyaṃ
apassanto Mahādevaṃ nāma amaccaṃ pucchi. so āha:
santi deva bahū paṇḍitā bhikkhū ti. taṃ sutvā rājā bhik-
khusaṃghassa bhattaṃ paṭiyādetvā bhattakiccāvasāne saṃ-
ghaṃ pucchi: gantabbaṃ nu kho bhante bhagavato bodhinā[^092-8]
Laṅkādīpaṃ no ti. saṃgho Moggaliputtatissattherassa bhā-
raṃ akāsi. thero gantabbaṃ mahārāja mahābodhinā Laṅ-
kādīpan ti vatvā bhagavato pañca mahāadhiṭṭhānāni kathesi.
katamāni pañca.
bhagavā kira mahāparinibbānamañce nipanno, Laṅkādīpe
mahābodhipatiṭṭhāpanatthāya Asahājāmahābodhi-
gaṇhaṇatthaṃ[^092-9] gamissati, tadā mahābodhissa dakkhiṇasākhā
sayam eva chijjitvā suvaṇṇakaṭāhe patiṭṭhātū 'ti adhiṭṭhāsi.
idam ekaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ. tattha patiṭṭhānakāle 'va,[^092-10] mahā-
bodhi himavalāhakagabbhaṃ pavisitvā[^092-11] tiṭṭhatū 'ti[^092-12] adhiṭ-

pattehi ca phalehi ca chabbaṇṇaraṃsiyo[^092-13] muñcatū[^092-14] 'ti adhiṭ-

karotū'ti adhiṭṭhāsi. imaṃ[^092-15] catuttham adhiṭṭhānaṃ. Laṅ-
kādīpamhi yeva me doṇamattadhātuyo[^092-16] Mahācetiyamhi

patiṭṭhānakāle buddhavesaṃ gahetvā vehāsaṃ uppatitvā[^093-1]
yamakapāṭihāriyaṃ karontū 'ti adhiṭṭhāsi. idaṃ pañcamaṃ
adhiṭṭhānaṃ.[^093-2]
rājā imāni pañca mahāadhiṭṭhānāni sutvā pasannacitto
Pāṭaliputtato yāva mahābodhi tāva maggaṃ paṭijaggāpetvā
suvaṇṇakaṭāhatthāya bahuṃ suvaṇṇaṃ nīharāpesi. tāvad
eva ca rañño cittaṃ ñatvā Vissakammadevaputto[^093-3] kammā-
ravaṇṇam nimminitvā purato[^093-4] aṭṭhāsi. rājā naṃ[^093-5] disvā, tā-
ta imaṃ[^093-6] suvaṇṇaṃ gahetvā kaṭāhaṃ karohīti āha. pamā-
naṃ deva jānathā[^093-7] 'ti. tvam eva tāta ñatvā karohi.[^093-8] sādhu
deva karissāmīti suvaṇṇaṃ gahetvā attano ānubhāvena
hatthena parimadditvā[^093-9] suvaṇṇakaṭāhaṃ nimmini navahat-
thaparikkhepaṃ pañcahatthubbedhaṃ tihatthavikkhambhaṃ
aṭṭhaṅgulabahalaṃ hatthisoṇḍapamāṇamukhavaṭṭim.[^093-10] atha
rājā sattayojanāyāmāni yojanavitthārāya[^093-11] mahatiyā senāya
Pāṭaliputtato nikkhamitvā ariyasaṃghaṃ ādāya mahābo-
dhisamīpaṃ agamāsi. senā samussitadhajapatākaṃ[^093-12] nānā-
ratanavicittaṃ anekālaṃkārapatimaṇḍitaṃ[^093-13] nānāvidhaku-

tato pupphagandhādīhi[^094-1] pūjetvā tikkhattuṃ padakkhiṇaṃ
katvā aṭṭhasu ṭhānesu vanditvā uṭṭhāya añjaliṃ paggayha
ṭhatvā saccavacanakiriyāya bodhiṃ gaṇhitukāmo bhūmito
yāva mahābodhissa dakkhiṇasākhā tāva uccaṃ katvā ṭhapi-
tassa sabbaratanamayapīṭhassa upari suvaṇṇakaṭāhaṃ ṭha-
pāpetvā ratanapīṭhaṃ āruyha suvaṇṇatūlikaṃ gahetvā
manosilāya lekhaṃ katvā, yadi mahābodhinā Laṅkādīpe
patiṭṭhātabbaṃ yadi cāhaṃ buddhasāsane nibbematiko
bhaveyyaṃ[^094-2] mahābodhi sayam eva imasmiṃ suvaṇṇakaṭāhe[^094-3]
patiṭṭhātū 'ti saccavacanakiriyam akāsi. saha saccakiri-
yāya bodhisākhā[^094-4] manosilāya paricchinnaṭṭhāne chijjitvā
gandhakalalapūrassa suvaṇṇakaṭāhassa upari aṭṭhāsi.[^094-5] tassa
ubbedhena dasahattho khandho hoti, catuhatthā catuhatthā
pañca mahāsākhā pañcahi eva phalehi patimaṇḍitā,[^094-6] khud-
dakasākhānaṃ pana sahassaṃ. atha rājā mūle lekhāya[^094-7]
upari tivaṅgule padese[^094-8] aññaṃ lekhaṃ paricchinditvā[^094-9] tato
tāvad eva bubbulakā[^094-10] hutvā dasa mahāmūlāni nikkhamiṃsu,
puna uparūpari tivaṅgule tivaṅgule aññā nava lekhā paric-
chindi.[^094-11] tāpi[^094-12] dasadasabubbulakā hutvā navuti mūlāni[^094-13]
nikkhamiṃsu. paṭhamakā dasa mahāmūlā caturaṅgula-
mattaṃ nikkhantā, itare pi[^094-14] gavakkhajālasadisaṃ anusib-
bantā nikkhantā. ettakaṃ pāṭihīraṃ[^094-15] rājā ratanapīṭha-
matthake ṭhito yeva disvā añjalim paggayha mahānādaṃ
nadi, anekāni bhikkhusahassāni sādhukāram akaṃsu,[^094-16] saka-

khandhasākhāpasākho pañcaphalapatimaṇḍito suvaṇṇaka-
